Sessions `adm1.<payload>.<hmac>` (12 h, signed with `AUTH_SECRET`, sent as `x-admin-token`); the role is re-read from `admins` (memoised one minute). `GET /api/admin/session` never 401s: `{ googleClientId, googleReady, passwordAllowed, me, schema, schemaWanted, databaseConfigured }`; `POST /api/admin/session { credential }` -> `{ token, me, expiresIn }` (`403 not_an_admin | email_not_verified`, `401 invalid_token`, `503 google_not_configured`). `adminGet` = any admin; `adminPost` = editor unless the route asks for owner. `GET/POST /api/admin/admins`: `invite`, `role`, `remove` (`409 last_owner`, `409 already_admin`). Every write leaves an `audit_log` row (actor = admin email; `owner` for recovery password and jobs); Change Log filters by admin, action and date range (India time).

### The shell (`app.js`)

Eight folding groups (only the current group starts open); **Find a page** filters the menu (Enter opens first match, Esc clears); top bar: breadcrumb, date range (Today/7/30/90/Custom) on `range: true` pages, global search **Ctrl K / Cmd K** (pages, settings, users, songs, quick actions), Refresh with an auto-refresh menu (15 s/30 s/1 min/5 min), profile menu with theme (Light/Dark/Match device) and time zone. Keyboard (press `?`): `/` search, `R` refresh, `T` theme, `[` collapse menu, `G` then letter: D Dashboard, L Listening Now, U All Users, F Feedback, S Top Songs, M Find Music, T Trending, I Search Overview, N Send Notification, H App Errors, A Smart Alerts, B Database Tables. Routes `#/<page id>?<params>`; `ctx.onLeave(fn)` guards unsaved changes.

Controls kit (`kit/controls.js`): `VX.me`, `VX.can('edit'|'owner')`, `ui.stats` (required ⓘ explanation), `ui.menu`, `ui.dropdown/radios/checks/slider/switchRow/chips`, `ui.sortable`, `ui.picker/userPicker/musicPicker`, date/time pickers (console time zone, IST default), `ui.colorPicker`, `ui.imageUpload` (the file is read as a `data:` address, since the console's `img-src` allows `'self' data: https:` and refuses `blob:`; cropped, stored by `POST /api/admin/upload` in the public `console-media` bucket), `ui.dataTable` (search, filters, sort, Columns, 25/50/100 rows, CSV/Excel export, bulk select), `VX.confirm({ typed: true })` for destructive actions.

**App Settings framework:** `VX.settingsPage({ id, title, sections })`; each section edits one `settings` key through `POST /api/admin/settings { key, value }` (null = built-in). Every section has a status chip (Live in app / Using built-in / Unsaved), **Save & apply** ("Users will see this change within 1 minute"), **Cancel**, **Reset to built-in**, optional live preview, **Change history** (last 20, **Restore this version**). Maintenance mode and forced updates require typing `CONFIRM`.

### Adding a console page

1. One job per page: create `frontend/public/admin/pages/<area>.js` calling `VX.page({ id, title, group, icon, order, desc, render, range, keywords, hidden })` (`group` is one of the eight sections in `GROUPS` in `app.js` and `order` is the page's place within that section, never shared with another page; `desc` is one plain sentence, required; icons are sprite ids without `i-`); add old addresses to `REDIRECTS`.
2. Add its `<script>` to `index.html` before `app.js`.
3. Add the Worker route under `backend/worker/functions/api/admin/` with `adminGet`/`adminPost`, register it in `backend/worker/index.ts`, add mocks to `frontend/e2e/support/console-mocks/`.

### Pages and their routes (key facts)

- **Dashboard:** `GET /api/admin/overview?days=` -> `{ configured, schema, days, kpis, timeline, platforms, versions, attention: [{ id, level: bad|warn|info, text, action, page, params }], checkedAt }`. Attention items (`attentionFor` in `api/admin/overview.ts`) name today's page ids with no `tab`: `db-maintenance`, `app-errors`, `feedback` with `params: { status: 'new' }` (opens `#/feedback?status=new`), `notification-setup`, `listen-together`, and `server-checks` for the `requests` item; the Dashboard waits at most 4 s for the meter, and no keys or no answer simply means no item. Twelve stat cards (sparklines from the last 14 days of `timeline`, plus Daily ÷ monthly) deep-link to pages with filters; four charts; **App versions in use** (from `versions`, grouped by app version, linking to App Versions); per-admin layout (Customise: drag, size, hide) in browser storage `vinax_admin_dash.<email>`.
- **Listening Now / Recent Activity / Rooms:** one call `GET /api/admin/live` -> `{ configured, now, counts, listening, feed (newest 60), rooms }`; signed-in people only (a device counts when its `account_id` is set); world map of places; Editor actions `end_room { code }`, `end_all_rooms` (typed CONFIRM).
- **All Users / profile:** `GET /api/admin/listeners` (`admin_users`: filters `q, platform, active, joined/joinedFrom/joinedTo, country, region, city, version, language, sort, limit (25/50/100), offset, ids`), `?filters=1`, `?account=<id>` (`user_profile`; `profile.extras`, `profile.signIns` last 100). POST: `block`/`unblock` (Editor; blocked accounts get `403 blocked` at sign-in and `401 signed_out` reason `blocked`), `signout_device`, `delete` (Owner; reason + CONFIRM), `notes` (tags/notes, audited). Top Listeners/At Risk: `?view=top&days=` / `?view=at-risk&quiet=7|14|30`.
- **Feedback:** `GET /api/admin/feedback` (status new/read/resolved/all, kind, platform, date); POST `status`, `reply` (push to the sender's signed-in devices; `409 no_account | no_device`, `400 push_not_configured`), `delete`, `delete_resolved`.
- **Music/People:** `GET /api/admin/analytics?view=music|rising|geo|engagement|listening|retention|funnel|skips|errors|devices|problems&days=`; Trending/search insights `GET /api/admin/trends?view=trending|search|pins&days=` (POST `pin`, `unpin`, `synonym`: at most 100 pins and 200 synonyms; `400 bad_query|bad_song|bad_synonym|too_many`; a pin reaches the app as `searchPins` in the client bundle and puts that song first for exactly matching words).
- **Songs With Problems** (Music, after Songs People Skip; follows the date range): `?view=problems` (`stats_song_problems`) -> totals as stat cards with info buttons, then two tables: songs that fail to play (errors, failed plays = "no sources left", people, last seen) and songs opened with no lyrics. Each row has **Block** (`VX.music.block` -> `/api/admin/content`, then a refresh; "Blocked" when already on the blocklist; disabled for viewers), **Find Music** and the song menu; a hidden Artist column makes artists searchable. An older database gets the one-time update card. Playback errors name the song from the first app after 1.16.2: `reportError(kind, message, song)` in `services/analytics/telemetry.ts` sends it in the event's song fields (the message is unchanged, so App Errors still groups by it; the dedupe key includes the song id; a `gen-` song is sent without it), so the page lists only reports from apps with that change.
- **Find Music / Blocked:** `GET /api/admin/catalog-search?q=&type=songs|albums|artists|playlists`; `GET/POST /api/admin/content` (`block` song/album/artist/keyword with reason Copyright/Wrong audio/Offensive/Other, `reason`, `unblock`); `/api/blocklist` returns `{ ids, artists, keywords, albums }`.
- **Notifications:** `GET/POST /api/admin/push` (audience filters: country > state > city, language, device kind, activity `today|active7|active30|heavy|quiet|away7|dormant14`, tags, chosen users, pasted emails (<= 200), channel; `dryRun`, `test` (own devices), `draft`, `when: 'schedule'` (1 minute to 60 days ahead), send now; `409 quiet_hours|kind_off|duplicate`); `GET/POST /api/admin/notifications` (`retract`, `cancel`, `delete`); `POST /api/push/opened { id }` counts taps. Only people signed in with Google are reached (`push_devices.account_id`). Scheduled sends: up to 3 due rows per run, claimed `scheduled -> sending`. **Notification Rules** (`push` setting): `{ minGapHours: 6|12|24|48, quiet: { enabled, start, end } (India time), types: { announcements, releases, recommendations, reminders } }`; saved audiences in `push-audiences` (<= 30).
- **Automatic Notifications:** weekly recap (Sunday from 7 PM in the person's time; once a week; needs 30 minutes of listening; opens `/stats`, the weekly report; the body ends "Tap for your weekly report."), win-back (daytime 9-21 their time, after 7 quiet days; once a week, none after three unanswered), new releases (within 1.5 days of a release by a top-three artist; one a day, never the same song twice). Switches in the `outreach` setting `{ recap, winback, release }`; `GET /api/admin/outreach`, `POST` `preview` (editor) / `run` (owner).
- **A/B Tests:** `GET/POST /api/admin/experiments` (`save`, `status draft|running|paused|finished`, `delete`); public `GET /api/experiments` lists running tests. Assignment is on the device (FNV-1a over `deviceId:key`; a second hash over `deviceId:key:in` against `percent`); live slots `home-shelf-order` (control / discovery-first) and `rec-next-songs` (control / taste-0.15 / taste-0.18). **Results:** `GET /api/admin/experiments?view=results&key=&days=` (1-90, default 30; `stats_experiment`; `400 bad_key`; `502 db_update_needed` on an older database) -> per version people, served, outcomes, complete / skip / early-skip / liked rates, plays per person and 7-day return (counted only for people first exposed over 7 days ago: did they use the app on a later India day within 7 days, on any of their devices); `enough` per version at 30 people and 100 outcomes, and at the top when two versions have it. The page's Results card (`RESULT_KEYS = ['rec-next-songs']` in `ab-tests.js`, pinned to the app's `REC_EXPERIMENT_KEYS` by a test) gives each non-draft measured test a days picker and a table per version, then either a too-few-people warning or which version was most played to the end; it says that other tests (`home-shelf-order`, the Home screen order) send no data on who saw which version. The End test dialog points to the Results.
- **App Health:** App Errors `GET /api/admin/errors` (grouped by kind + message shape via `_error_shape`; statuses new/investigating/fixed/ignored in `error_status`; clear = owner + CONFIRM), Server Checks `/api/admin/edge` plus the request meter card from `GET /api/admin/quota` (any admin, no database; `{ configured: false, missing, allowance }` -> a setup card with three steps, the `wrangler secret put` commands and a button to API Keys Status; `{ configured: true, requests, errors, subrequests, allowance, share, level, since, resetsAt, checkedAt, cached, byScript, byStatus }` -> used, left, errors, when it starts again, a meter, a warning from 80% and an urgent notice from 95%, splits by Worker and by outcome; `502 { configured: true, error: 'cf_unauthorized'|'cf_timeout'|'cf_failed', message }` -> "Could not load"), Music Sources `/api/admin/musicapi`, AI Assistant `/api/admin/ai` ([the providers card](#the-consoles-providers-card): models with the 30-day **Thumbs up** column, and the **Web search** card whose **Test search** calls `POST /api/admin/ai-search`, editors and owners), App Versions `/api/admin/releases`, API Keys Status `/api/admin/envcheck` (`Set` / `Missing` only, never a value; also lists `SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, optional `CRON_SECRET`, and `CF_ANALYTICS_TOKEN`, `CF_ACCOUNT_ID` under "Daily request meter").
- **Smart Alerts** (`alerts` job, every 5 min): one short push per problem to chosen admins' phones. Rules in owner-only `alert-rules`: `{ enabled, who: owners|editors|all, cooldownMinutes (60 default; 15-1440), quiet: { enabled, start '23:00', end '07:00' }, rules: { errorsSpike: { on, minErrors 10, factor 2 }, siteDown, noPlays: { on, minutes 60, onlyIfUsualPlays }, newFeedback: { on, minCount 1 }, jobsFailing: { on }, quotaHigh: { on, percent 80 (50/70/80/90/95) } } }`. `jobsFailing`: a switched-on job (not the alerts job, which cannot report itself) whose last two finished runs both failed (a run still going is ignored), or that is running late (`isOverdue`); the body names the job and the last error, or how long it has not run. `quotaHigh`: today's Worker requests reached `percent` of the daily allowance; judged only when `CF_ANALYTICS_TOKEN` and `CF_ACCOUNT_ID` are set (otherwise left out of `checked`; `GET /api/admin/alerts` answers `meter: { ready }` and the page says how to add the keys); at most once per UTC day. Links: `#/app-errors`, `#/server-checks` (siteDown, quotaHigh), `#/listening-now`, `#/feedback`, `#/scheduled-jobs`. An admin's phones are the active `push_devices` of the VinaX account with the same Google email (sign in to the app on the phone and allow notifications). Each alert is a `notifications` row (`kind 'system'`, `dedupe_key 'alert:<rule>:<cooldown>:<bucket>'`, for `quotaHigh` `'alert:quotaHigh:day:<YYYY-MM-DD>'` in UTC); quiet hours hold every rule except `siteDown` and `quotaHigh` (the allowance resets at 05:30 India time, so a held warning would arrive too late); alerts do not count toward the listener frequency cap.
- **Database pages:** Tables (`GET /api/admin/database`: friendly label, exact/approximate rows, size, `keptFor`, `schemaWanted`), Scheduled Jobs (`job_setting`, `run_job`; "running late" from `isOverdue`), Change Log (`GET /api/admin/audit?actor=&action=&from=&to=`), Maintenance (database update card, clean up old data, rebuild statistics, close empty rooms, forget silent phones; destructive actions typed CONFIRM).
- **Growth & SEO:** Google Search, Page Check, Search Console. Bots card: `_lib/robots.ts` + `robots` setting `{ bots: { "<bot>": true|false } }`; `GET /api/robots` builds the text; robots.txt itself stays a **static file** (`frontend/scripts/robots-from-api.mjs`, last build step, fetches it on host builds); saving calls `POST /api/admin/seo { action: 'publish_robots' }` which POSTs the `PAGES_DEPLOY_HOOK` secret. Page Check shows a 12-page sample; its **Check every page** button asks `GET /api/admin/seo?view=all&part=N` until the last part and lists every page of `sitemap-static.xml` and `sitemap-hubs.xml` (40 pages per request, under the free plan's 50 sub-requests; song, album and artist pages are sampled, not read in full).

### Published settings (the `settings` table; `SETTING_KEYS` in `_lib/settings.ts`)

| Key | Written by (console page) | Read by |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `banners` (<= 10 live; song/album/playlist/artist/charts/page link; dates) | Home Screen | `activeBanners` in `_lib/clientConfig.ts` -> `PromoBanner`. The page list (`BANNER_PAGES`) no longer has Explore or Mixes; a saved banner whose page is `/explore` opens `/discover` and one with `/mixes` opens `/made-for-you` (`BANNER_PAGE_MOVED`; the console's link picker, `linkFromPath` in `settings-home.js`, does the same) |
| `greeting`, `broadcast` (top message bar, id derived from words/link/style), `announcement` (pop-up, `ts` content hash), `home-layout` `{ order, hidden }` (12 sections) | Home Screen | `publicClientConfig`, `AnnouncementPopup`, `BroadcastBar` |
| `flags` `{ listenTogether: false }` | Features & AI | `flagOn` |
| `vinax-ai` | Features & AI | [VinaX AI](#vinax-ai). An old `rec-config` or `rec-config-history` row is ignored: no weight overrides reach the app |
| `min-version` `{ android\|web\|ios: { minVersion, mode: suggest\|force }, message, build? }` | App Updates | `publicVersionPolicy`; `/api/version` adds `minVersion`/`updateMode`; app `VersionNotice` |
| `site-mode` `{ mode: readonly\|maintenance, note }`, `maintenance-window`, `status-note` `{ enabled, text, level }` | Maintenance | `/api/site-mode`, router (`_lib/readOnly.ts`: read-only refuses POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE on `/api/feedback`, `/api/account/data`, `/api/profile`, `/api/follow`, `/api/shared-playlist`, `/api/username` with `503 read_only`), `SiteGate`, `/api/status` |
| `search-synonyms`, `search-pins`, `catalog-sources` (priority + on/off), `language-order` | Search & Music Sources | client bundle -> app search / `activeBases()` / Home language rail |
| `support-faq` `[{ q, a, category, enabled }]` (<= 30) | Help Center | Help page |
| `retention` `{ eventsDays (7-3650, default 180), errorsDays, feedbackDays, notificationsDays }` | Data & Privacy | housekeeping |
| `push`, `push-audiences`, `outreach`, `email`, `alert-rules`, `jobs`, `robots` | Notifications / System | their jobs and routes |

Limits: dates and "today" follow India's calendar on the server; users per version on A/B Tests is an estimate; A/B results exist only for next-song tests (`rec-next-songs`), and each version needs at least 30 people and 100 songs played before a winner is suggested; existing phones join the notification audience only after they register again while signed in; blocking a user stops new sign-ins and session checks at once.

### Console tests

`frontend/src/__tests__/adminConsole.test.ts` loads the real `core.js`, kit and every page into a DOM (index.html loads every file in order under the strict CSP; unique page ids in the eight sections with icon and one-sentence description and no tabs; every section's sidebar order pinned, no two pages in one place, no `GROUP_MOVES`; every old address redirects to an existing page; every `go('<id>')` names an existing page; banned words; no markup from strings; every page renders from empty and failing answers without `undefined`, `NaN` or `[object Object]`). `frontend/e2e/console-shell.spec.ts` and `console-pages.spec.ts` (mocks in `e2e/support/console-mocks/`), `consoleTrends.test.ts`, `consoleProblems.test.ts` (Songs With Problems), `services/search/pins.test.ts`, and `backend/worker/__tests__/console*.test.ts` (Auth, People, Music, Engage, SettingsA, SettingsB, System, Alerts, Rules), `requestMeter.test.ts` (the meter, its cache and `/api/admin/quota`), `email.test.ts`, `adminApi.test.ts`.

## Android

The Android app is a thin Capacitor shell around the web app (`frontend/capacitor.config.ts`: `appId` `app.Vinax.music`, `webDir` `dist`, `server.url` the production site so web changes reach installed apps without reinstall, `server.androidScheme` `https`, `android.allowMixedContent` `false` - do not relax the last two). Risks recorded in that config and still standing: whoever controls the origin controls the code every installed app runs, and an origin outage leaves only what the service worker cached (the long-term fix - bundle `dist/` and use the origin for a verified update check - is not implemented). Native code is limited to what a web view cannot do.

### How a package is built and released

| | Build Android APK | Release APK |
| --- | --- | --- |
| File | `.github/workflows/buildapk.yml` | `.github/workflows/release.yml` |
| Trigger | Dispatched by Auto release on every merge to `main`, or manual | A pushed tag matching `v*` |
| `versionCode` | `1000 + run number` | `1000000 + run number` |
| `versionName` | `version` in `frontend/package.json` | same |
| `VITE_BUILD_NUMBER` | the `versionCode` | the raw run number |
| Debug package | built, artifact `vinax-debug-apk` (kept 2 days) | not built |
| Signed package | only when the keystore secret exists | always; fails without the keystore |
| Published as | latest release tagged `v<version>-build<versionCode>` | latest release on the tag |
| Files | `vinax.apk`, `vinax.apk.sha256` | same |

Steps (in `frontend/`): compute `versionCode`; `npm run build` with `VITE_BUILD_NUMBER`; Java 21 + Android SDK; `npx cap add android`, `npx cap sync android`, `node scripts/patch-android.js`; write `versionCode`/`versionName` into `android/app/build.gradle`; build (release signing via injected properties from the decoded keystore); copy to `vinax.apk`, write SHA-256, publish. Rules: a debug-signed package is never published as a release; in Build Android APK a push with no keystore skips the release with a notice (green), while a manual dispatch or a commit message containing `[release]` fails without it; **the release body's `VersionCode:` line is read by `/api/version` - do not remove it**; the two `versionCode` ranges are disjoint and the update check compares build numbers only, so a phone on a tag build (> 1,000,000) is not offered later `main` builds - once listeners have a tag build, later packages for them must come from tags; debug builds use the public `frontend/ci/debug-keystore.b64` which must never sign a release; no workflow sets `VINAX_PLAY_STORE_BUILD`, so every CI package is the sideload variant with `REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES`.

### Building locally

`frontend/android/` is generated and git-ignored; never edit it. Needs Node 22+, Java 21 JDK, Android SDK. `npm run cap:add:android` (first time), `npm run cap:sync` (after web or native changes), `npm run android:debug` -> `android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk`. A local build keeps `versionCode` 1, so the update check offers the published build.

### What `scripts/patch-android.js` does (idempotent, after every sync)

1. Copies `frontend/native-android/*.java` into the package folder (placeholder `__PKG__` -> `app.Vinax.music`).
2. Copies `native-android/res/**` (widget layouts, vector icons, drawables, provider info, `values/vinax_widgets.xml`) into the app resources; every file is prefixed `vinax_`.
3. Copies `native-android/google-services.json` into `android/app/` (the push build plugin applies itself when present).
4. Writes `MainActivity.java`: registers `VinaxMediaPlugin`, `AudioRoutePlugin`, `VinaxPushPlugin`, `DiscoPlugin` and `VinaxCastPlugin` before `super.onCreate` (app-local plugins are not auto-discovered; registering touches no Cast class), turns shared text into a VinaX link (`shareAsLink`, before `super.onCreate` and `super.onNewIntent`; see [Native layer](#native-layer)), relays notification taps that ask for the full-screen player, calls the web view's `onResume()`/`resumeTimers()` in both `onPause` and `onStop` (so controls act in the background), sets `allowSignInCheck` WebView settings for Turnstile.
5. Patches `AndroidManifest.xml`: foreground-service, media-playback foreground-service, wake-lock, notification, microphone and `MODIFY_AUDIO_SETTINGS` permissions; `REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES` unless `VINAX_PLAY_STORE_BUILD=1`; car-app metadata, media-button receiver, `VinaxMediaService` (exported, type `mediaPlayback`, media-browser intent filter), both widget receivers, the `com.google.android.gms.cast.framework.OPTIONS_PROVIDER_CLASS_NAME` meta-data naming `app.Vinax.music.VinaxCastOptionsProvider` (an older copy is removed first), and after `MainActivity`'s launcher filter one `<!-- vinax-links -->` ... `<!-- /vinax-links -->` block: a VIEW filter for www with `autoVerify="true"`, one for the apex without it (both limited to `APP_LINK_PATHS` by `pathPrefix`) and a SEND `text/plain` filter. Each run first removes that block, or the 1.13 single unverified filter, then inserts it again, so a second run or an old patched project ends with exactly one set (`patchAndroidLinks.test.ts`). Exits with an error rather than write a manifest without the media service if it cannot find `</application>`.
6. Patches `app/build.gradle`: `androidx.media`, `firebase-messaging` and `com.google.android.gms:play-services-cast-framework:22.3.1` dependencies (the last brings `androidx.mediarouter`, the device list, and needs minSdk 23; the app's is 24); debug builds signed with the committed debug keystore.
7. Copies launcher icons, background colour and `xml/automotive_app_desc.xml` from `frontend/android-res/`.

After upgrading `@capacitor/android` (8.4.1 at last check) run the patch once and read its log.

### Native layer

| File (`frontend/native-android/`) | Role |
| --- | --- |
| `VinaxMediaService.java` | Foreground service owning the media session, media-style notification, lock-screen controls; extends the media-browser service base so car/watch clients can browse |
| `VinaxMediaPlugin.java` | Capacitor bridge, registered as `VinaxMedia` |
| `VinaxPlayerWidget.java` (+ `res/layout/vinax_widget_player.xml`, `_tall.xml`) | Now Playing widget (4x1 default, resizable; card layout from ~2 rows) |
| `VinaxQuickPlayWidget.java` | Static "Play my mix" widget (one tap opens the app with `?widget=play`) |
| `AudioRoutePlugin.java` | `VinaxAudioRoute`: VinaX AI Live voice on loudspeaker or earpiece |
| `DiscoPlugin.java` | `VinaxDisco`: the flashlight on the beat, full brightness and no system bars while disco lights are open (see [Disco lights](#disco-lights)) |
| `VinaxCastPlugin.java` | `VinaxCast`: casting to a TV from the app. Methods `init`, `showDevicePicker`, `loadMedia`, `play`, `pause`, `seek`, `setVolume`, `endSession`; events `availability` (`devices`), `session` (`connected`, `deviceName`), `status` (`state`, `idleReason`, `position`, `duration`, `url`; on every receiver update and once a second). Nothing Cast runs when the plugin loads: `init` (the first time Connect to a device opens) checks for Play services, then starts the cast framework; any failure answers `{ available: false }`, so a phone without Play services simply has no casting. All calls run on the main thread. The device picker is the system media-route chooser; while casting it opens the TV's card with Stop casting. **Written against the 22.3.1 library classes but not yet compiled or run on a device** |
| `VinaxCastOptionsProvider.java` | Cast settings read from the manifest: the Default Media Receiver (the same as the website); no cast notification and no cast media session, so `VinaxMediaService` keeps the notification, lock screen and media buttons; ending the session stops the TV |
| `google-services.json` | Firebase config for FCM push |

Audio is played by the web player's `<audio>` element inside the web view; the service mirrors state and sends commands back. Bridge (`frontend/src/services/media-session/index.ts`): web -> native `setMetadata` (title first, 256 px JPEG artwork data URL; lock-screen lyric line travels in the artist field), `setPlaybackState`, `setPosition` (<= once a second), `provideChildren`, `stop`; native -> web `action` (play, pause, next, previous, seek, +/-10 s, open player, `resync`) and `requestChildren`. `mediaSessionAvailable()` in `services/native/index.ts`; the module keeps the last 12 bridge calls (`getMediaSessionLog()`) and `runNotificationSelfTest()`. Android 13+ needs the notification permission (asked once at launch, again at first play). Playback resumes by itself after a phone call (within half an hour).

**Background playback** relies on: web view never paused (`onResume`/`resumeTimers` re-asserted); the service enters the foreground synchronously in `onStartCommand` with type `mediaPlayback` (Android 14 requirement) and stays foreground while paused; after start the plugin calls the service directly; if a start is refused the plugin rejects with `fgs-start-not-allowed`, posts a plain notification when possible and asks the web view for `resync` on next resume (audio keeps playing, controls arrive late); `ACTION_AUDIO_BECOMING_NOISY` pauses at once (receiver not exported); channel `IMPORTANCE_LOW`, category `transport`; media buttons via the exported receiver. **Limits:** playback does not survive process death (swipe-away tears the service down; the widget's play then relaunches with `?widget=play`); nothing can start playback while the process is dead; audio focus is not managed natively; widgets' look is fixed (`res/values/vinax_widgets.xml`: black translucent `#E6000000` with a faint green hairline, a green `#1ED760` play disc with a black icon, `#1F1F1F` art slot, `#B3B3B3` muted text) and does not follow style/theme; there is no light variant yet.

**VinaX links and Share to VinaX.** The www VIEW filter is verified against `https://www.vinaxmusic.online/.well-known/assetlinks.json` (`frontend/public/.well-known/assetlinks.json`, the release key's SHA-256 only), so a shared song, playlist or invite opens straight in the app; the apex filter is unverified (see [Listen Together](#listen-together), Website to app). A debug-signed package is never verified, since the CI debug keystore is public and its fingerprint is deliberately not listed. The SEND filter puts VinaX in the system share sheet for text: `MainActivity.shareAsLink` rewrites the intent in place into a VIEW intent for `https://www.vinaxmusic.online/share?text=...&title=...` (`EXTRA_TEXT` capped at 8,000 characters without splitting a surrogate pair; `EXTRA_SUBJECT` as the title when it is 200 characters or fewer; inside try/catch). It runs before `super.onCreate`, so the bridge keeps it as the launch link for `App.getLaunchUrl`, and before `super.onNewIntent`, so `@capacitor/app` fires `appUrlOpen`; `AppLayout`'s `openDeepLink` / `deepLinkPath` then routes it inside the page with no reload and no cut in playback (`loadUrl`, which push links use, would reload the web view). The quick-play widget's `?widget=play` intent is explicit and needs no filter. Not yet compiled or run on a device.

**App styles, themes and system bars:** styles are web CSS and arrive with the live site. The `<html>` background and `theme-color` meta follow `TEMPLATE_CANVAS` (Black theme `#000000`). No native code sets status/navigation bar colour; Capacitor's default picks bar icons from the device's dark mode, not VinaX's theme (unverified on a device). The page uses `viewport-fit=cover` and `env(safe-area-inset-*)`.

**Downloads** (`frontend/src/services/downloads/index.ts`; native below, the web after it): on Android audio is fetched with native HTTP; walks listed bitrate variants from highest down (a variant missing or under 10 KB moves on; dead network/stall/full disk stops); creates `vinax-downloads/` before each attempt; 15 s connect, 30 s read, 5-minute cap; files in the app's internal data directory; saved once on disk, then copied into the audio cache in the background (3 MB slices, 2-minute cap, rebuilt on launch if missing); failure reasons `offline | unavailable | storage | timeout | unknown` (bulk download stops on `offline`); offline sources tried in order: `blob:` from the audio cache, service-worker `/offline-audio/<id>` (Range support, cache `vinax-audio-v1`), native file URL; entry points song menu, collection pages, Favorites, `/offline`; with no network and a saved song the router starts on `/offline`. Boot recovery never deletes downloads (skips `vinax-audio-v1` and runs only after a request to the origin succeeds). Downloads are not in backups. Smart downloads keep this week's Weekly Mix on Wi-Fi (Android only).

**Saving songs on the web and iPhone/iPad (1.16).** `canSaveOffline()` (`index.ts`) is true in the Android app and in any browser with the Cache API (`webSaveSupported()`); the song menu shows **Download** whenever it is. Web saves go to `services/downloads/web.ts`, loaded by `import()` only on the first save or when saves exist at boot (nothing new in the first load). Same bitrate ladder as Android (`downloadUrls`, Download quality first); a CORS fetch (`mode: 'cors'`, `credentials: 'omit'`, 5-minute cap per attempt) from `aac.saavncdn.com`, which answers `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` (checked 2026-11-17; the Worker's `/api/cat/stream` 302s carry it too); error pages, non-audio and tiny bodies are refused and an opaque answer is never stored. The song goes into `vinax-audio-v1` at `/offline-audio/<id>` with content-type, content-length and accept-ranges, which `sw.js` already serves with Range support, and the `downloadsStore` entry keeps the real `bytes`. A host that refuses CORS (a no-cors probe reaches it) answers **Not available to save** (failure `blocked`, ends the ladder); one that cannot be reached is `offline`; hosts outside the CSP `connect-src` (`*.saavncdn.com`, `*.vinaxmusic.online`, same origin) are refused without a request. Storage: a save is refused below 50 MB free (`navigator.storage.estimate`), `navigator.storage.persist()` is asked once on the first save of a session, a one-time toast says when saves pass 1 GB, and on iPhone/iPad outside the Home Screen a toast suggests Add to Home Screen (Safari can clear a site's storage after about a week without a visit). `checkWebSaves()` at boot drops entries whose cache copy the browser cleared. A saved song plays from `/offline-audio/<id>` while the service worker controls the page, else it streams once and keeps a `blob:` copy. Smart downloads and the collection/Favorites **Download all** buttons stay Android-only (a whole playlist can be about 1 GB in a browser). `/offline` shows exact sizes for web saves (Android's stay "≈"), the browser's "X used · Y free for VinaX", **Remove all** (with a confirm, both platforms) and, on iPhone, the Home Screen tip with **How** (the install guide).

**Voice:** voice search and Drive-mode commands use the system recogniser via `@capacitor-community/speech-recognition` (`features/voice/stt.ts`; the web view's constructor is never created). Live voice: see [Live voice in the Android app](#live-voice-in-the-android-app).

**Push:** `services/push/nativePush.ts` registers the device and routes taps; it does nothing without `google-services.json` and the Worker's `FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT`. The key inside `google-services.json` ships in every package, so it is public and safe only with restrictions in Google Cloud: Application restrictions -> Android apps, package `app.Vinax.music` bound to the release keystore SHA-1 (and the CI debug keystore SHA-1 if debug APKs should talk to Firebase); API restrictions -> only Firebase Installations, FCM Registration and Firebase Cloud Messaging APIs (project `vinax-music`, number `152181244509`). Rotating the key cuts push for installed apps until they update.

### Updates (`frontend/src/services/update.ts`)

1. `AppLayout` calls `checkForUpdate()` at launch and on every resume; it reads the installed `versionCode` natively and fetches `/api/version` with native HTTP, comparing build numbers only.
2. `/api/version` reads the latest release with a server-side token: `{ build, version, apkUrl, sha256, minBuild }` (`build` from the `VersionCode:` line, else the tag's `-build<n>` suffix; `sha256` from the published `.sha256` file; `apkUrl` is the Worker's `/api/apk` which streams the package; `/apk` redirects to it; cached 60 s).
3. A newer build opens `UpdateDialog` (Update now / Update later; later snoozes that build 24 h; Escape/back count as later; the Settings manual check ignores the snooze).
4. `minBuild` (console Minimum App Version / `min-version`) makes the update mandatory with no Later; a failed resume check never clears a known update (`mergeResumeCheck`).
5. **Update now** (`downloadAndInstall()`): native download, SHA-256 computed before anything is written; missing/malformed hash or mismatch aborts; then the file goes to the app cache and the system installer (consent shown first time).
6. The installer reports nothing back; `markUpdateAttempt`/`installLikelyBlocked()` switch the dialog to one-time reinstall guidance when it returns for the same build after > 10 minutes (e.g. different signing key).

### What cannot be verified without a device

Unit tests cover update attempt tracking, download retry/fallback, the media-session web side, release parsing; the Java in `native-android/` is never compiled by the test toolchain (`nativeMediaService.test.ts` pins its contracts at string level); CI compiles the generated project but runs nothing (the cast plugin and options provider have not been through that build yet either: no package with them has been built or installed). Needs a real phone ([Real-device QA script](#real-device-qa-script)): install on a given Android version and the patched manifest; notification/lock-screen controls and artwork on Android 12-15 including late controls and resync; vendor battery policies; headphone-unplug pause; resume after calls, headset/wireless buttons, audio focus; car/watch browser; both widgets on light/dark wallpapers and with the app swiped away; verified links (`adb shell pm get-app-links app.Vinax.music`; a shared song link opens the app with no chooser on Android 12+) and Share to VinaX from another app on a cold and a warm start; system-bar colours per style/theme; downloads (storage, three offline sources, offline launch, full storage); web saves in Safari on iPhone/iPad and from the Home Screen (no browser test has saved a song yet; vitest covers it with Cache API and fetch fakes); the update flow end to end (hash check, installer consent, different-signature reinstall, mandatory gate); background push with the app closed; casting from the app (pick a TV, the song hands over with the phone muted, pause and seek from the TV remote, Stop casting from the device card, a phone without Play services shows no casting); microphone prompt and recogniser; haptics, back button, safe-area insets, offline first launch.

## Android push setup

Notifications reach the Android app while fully closed once the owner sets the Worker secret `FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT`. Until then the app runs normally and tokens are stored, but no message is sent (`fcmConfigured()` in `_lib/fcm.ts`).

1. In the push provider's console open the project named in `frontend/native-android/google-services.json` > Project settings > Service accounts > generate a new private key (JSON downloads).
2. `cd backend && npx wrangler secret put FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT --config worker/wrangler.toml` and paste the whole JSON. No app rebuild needed.

**The project (12.2):** app `app.Vinax.music` in the project **VinaX Music** (`vinax-music`, project number `152181244509`, free plan); `google-services.json` is that project's file; the same project runs Google sign-in. The Android app is registered under the CI signing key's SHA-1 `71:27:BB:E2:25:1F:9E:A4:89:61:EC:9B:76:A5:07:5F:94:DE:A2:CC`. **Published APKs are signed with the release key**, fingerprints (read from release v13.7.2, build 1427):

- SHA-1 `D9:55:85:C3:D4:4F:52:92:47:39:7C:95:A0:E5:F9:D1:11:BA:62:BE`
- SHA-256 `8C:AF:24:E7:AA:83:B7:EA:12:E1:2C:E3:FF:C1:14:DD:8C:EB:57:38:25:AB:EB:82:3E:FE:AE:E4:F2:6B:7C:CE`

The same SHA-256 is published in `frontend/public/.well-known/assetlinks.json` for Android App Links; if the release key ever changes, update it there too. The CI debug keystore's fingerprint is deliberately not listed there: that keystore is public (`frontend/ci/debug-keystore.b64`), so listing it would let anyone's APK take over VinaX links.

Add both to the Android app in Firebase (Project settings > Your apps > `app.Vinax.music` > Add fingerprint) and the SHA-1 to an Android OAuth client for `app.Vinax.music` in Google Cloud (APIs & Services > Credentials). If the Firebase API key is restricted to Android apps, the release SHA-1 must be on that restriction too. Without them, installed apps cannot get a push token or sign in with Google. Afterwards download the new `google-services.json` and replace `frontend/native-android/google-services.json`. Read any APK's fingerprint: `apksigner verify --print-certs vinax.apk`.

**How a push looks (13.7.3):** every message uses channel `vinax_picks` ("Song picks & news", high importance, created at app start), small icon `vinax_ic_notify` (white V), accent `#FFA42E`; the manifest names the same three as FCM defaults; a push while the app is open is shown too (`presentationOptions` in `capacitor.config.ts`). These are compiled into the APK (apply from the first APK after 13.7.3).

The old project (`apptarangmusic`, app `app.tarang.music`) is retired (its devices reported `FIS_AUTH_ERROR`); delete its keys or the project once the new key is set. Because the package changed, the 12.2 APK installs beside the old app; listeners bring their library across by signing in with the same account (or with a backup file).

**The message (1.12):** every FCM message carries `android.priority: high`, `ttl: 86400s`, `direct_boot_ok`, a `PRIORITY_HIGH` / `PUBLIC` notification on the `vinax_picks` channel, and the title and body in `data` too, so a closed app shows it at once; if a push still arrives only when the app opens, the cause is operational — the console's Notifications > setup check says whether `FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` is set and accepted and whether the release fingerprints are registered (`/api/admin/fcmcheck`). The app also reads the `announcement` setting on open, which is why a push sent while the key is missing appears at the next open.

**Native push (1.12.7):** `frontend/native-android/VinaxPushService.java` is a `FirebaseMessagingService` that takes `MESSAGING_EVENT` ahead of the Capacitor plugin's (manifest `android:priority="1"`): on `onNewToken` and at every app start (`VinaxPushPlugin.load()` → `FirebaseMessaging.getToken()`) it POSTs the token to `/api/push/fcm-register` from Java, with the signed-in session when the page handed one over (`VinaxPush.setSession`), so a phone has a push address whether or not the page, its permission flow or usage consent ever ran (before 1.12.7 no Android phone had ever registered). `onMessageReceived` draws a push that arrives with the app open (or a data-only push) with the white V small icon, the accent colour and the colour logo (`res/drawable/vinax_ic_large.png`) or the owner's picture as the large icon; a tap opens the push's `link` inside the app (`MainActivity.maybeOpenPushLink`). The page's `services/push/nativePush.ts` reads the same token through `VinaxPush.getToken()` and uploads it linked to the account; the old Capacitor plugin path remains as the fallback for APKs built before 1.12.7. `patch-android.js` registers the plugin, declares the service and adds `com.google.firebase:firebase-messaging` to the app module.

**Wired in the repository:** `frontend/scripts/patch-android.js` copies `google-services.json`; `frontend/src/services/push/nativePush.ts` registers on launch, posts the token to `/api/push/fcm-register` and routes taps; the Worker stores the token in `push_devices` (channel `fcm`) via `functions/api/push/fcm-register.ts`, signs/sends with `_lib/fcm.ts`; the console's push send fans out to stored tokens; dependency `@capacitor/push-notifications`. A device row holds the token, platform, app language, time-zone offset and coarse edge location (no name, username or install id). **Test delivery:** install the newest package, grant notification permission, swipe the app closed, send a push from the console; it should arrive and open the target.

## VinaX AI

The chat assistant at `/vinaxai` (page `frontend/src/pages/VinaXAIPage.tsx` -> `src/features/vinaxai/`). Recommendations, Radio and Up Next are still decided on the device; VinaX AI is the one place a signed-in listener talks to an AI. The listener's guide is [VinaX AI in the Listener guide](#vinax-ai-1).

### How a message travels

```text
VinaXAIPage (lazy, full screen inside AppLayout)
  features/vinaxai/store.ts -- POST /api/ai/chat (Bearer session) --> functions/api/ai/chat.ts
        session, rate limit, kill switch, body cap; conversation tree, daily count, memory and account read together
        from the start (each Supabase round trip is 300-500 ms); a new chat's row and the question
        saved while the model starts (the reply is saved only if it was; else an `error` and `saved: false`);
        system prompt (_lib/ai/prompt.ts); Auto ladder or the picked model (_lib/ai/router.ts)
        -> _lib/ai/adapters/* -> provider (Groq, Gemini, OpenRouter, NVIDIA, Workers AI)
  <-- text/event-stream: meta, title, [the provider's own lines, passed through], error, sources, done;
      reply saved with ctx.waitUntil when it ends; one cut short (Stop, a broken stream) is saved by the page (POST /api/ai/keep)
```

Every provider call goes through the Worker; no key, upstream host or raw upstream error reaches the browser; logs carry only route, model, provider, ladder, latency, status and token counts, never message text.

### Providers and secrets

| Secret / binding | Provider | Used for |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `GROQ_API_KEY` | Groq (OpenAI-compatible) | GPT-OSS 120B (code ladder first); dictation (Whisper); Read aloud (Orpheus) |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY` | Google Gemini (`streamGenerateContent?alt=sse`, key in `x-goog-api-key`) | Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite (long ladder, titles), Gemini 3.6/3.8 Flash and Gemma 4 (picked by hand), Live voice, dictation/Read aloud fallbacks |
| `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` | OpenRouter | `:free` code models and mirrors (`HTTP-Referer: https://www.vinaxmusic.online`, `X-Title: VinaX AI`) |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY` | NVIDIA NIM | Nemotron 3.5 Lightning, Mistral Large 4, Super VL and Bolt: most ladders (reasoning, vision, fast and music) |
| `AI` binding | Cloudflare Workers AI | Picked-by-hand chat models only (never in a ladder); Create image (FLUX.1 schnell) |
| `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` | Anthropic Claude (native Messages API, `x-api-key` + `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01`) | Claude Haiku 5.5 and Claude Haiku 4.5 (Haiku only, the owner's choice, 2026-11-12; Opus, Sonnet and Fable were taken out; Claude 3.5 Haiku and Claude 3 Haiku are retired at Anthropic, 2026-02-19 and 2026-04-20, so they are not offered): picked by hand only, never in a ladder, and only for the accounts in `_lib/ai/claudeAccess.ts` (see Claude below). Paid per token on the owner's key |
| `SEARCH_API_KEY`, vars `SEARCH_API_PROVIDER`, `SEARCH_DAILY_LIMIT` | Tavily or Serper | Web search for models that cannot ground |

Any one is enough. With none, `/api/ai/*` answers `503 ai_not_configured` and the page says "VinaX AI is unavailable right now".

### The registry

`backend/worker/functions/_lib/ai/models.ts` is the only place that names a model id. Each entry: id `<provider>:<upstream id>`, display name, picker group (`fast`, `balanced`, `smart`, `vision`, `code`, `claude`, `more`, `cloudflare`, or none for web-search-only), tier, flags (`vision`, `pdf`, `grounding`, `reasoning`, `artifacts`, `systemRole`, `thinkFormat`), `maxContextChars`, `maxOutput`, extra `params` (+ `highParams` for Extended thinking on models `thinking.ts` has no rule for), `firstEventMs`. Thinking levels are not in the registry: `thinking.ts` derives them from provider and upstream id, so they follow whatever models the approved list keeps.

**Approved models only (owner's list, 2026-11-12).** `APPROVED_MODELS` in `models.ts` is the allowlist: `registryFor` and `pickerRegistryFor` (`_lib/ai/config.ts`) and the console probe drop anything else, so `GET /api/ai/models` and every ladder use only these models, and a picked id outside the approved registry gets `400 unknown_model`. A model joins only by its id in `APPROVED_MODELS`: **model discovery was removed on 2026-11-17** (`_lib/ai/discovery.ts`, its six-hourly reads of the Groq, Gemini, NVIDIA and Workers AI model lists, the found models and the `more-<provider>` picker groups are gone). An old chat whose reply came from a model no longer in the registry still shows a readable name made from its id (`prettyName`, `_lib/ai/modelName.ts`). **Paused** (`PAUSED_MODELS`): 12 approved ids that failed the live check of the approved list after it went live the same day (timed out at 25 s: DeepSeek V4.1 Flash and GLM 5.3 Flash on NVIDIA, MiniMax M3.1 Flash, Gemini 3.8 Flash; answered with an error: Gemini 3.6 Flash, Gemma 4 31B on NVIDIA and Gemini, Gemma 4 26B on Gemini, GLM 5.3 Flash and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash on Workers AI; busy: the two Gemma 4 `:free` models on OpenRouter) keep their registry entries but are offered nowhere; DeepSeek V4.1 Flash had been Auto's first choice, so every Auto message waited 12 s for it. 21 approved models remain (13 answered; the 8 Workers AI ones were busy because the day's free neurons were spent, which resets at 00:00 UTC). The console's Health → VinaX AI table marks them **Paused** and its **Test** button still works on them (`POST /api/admin/ai` takes approved and paused ids), so you can see when one answers again; move its id back into `APPROVED_MODELS` then. A test checks that `MODELS` is exactly the approved and paused lists. NVIDIA and Workers AI ids were matched against each provider's live list that day; Groq and Gemini ids are the owner's (no key on the build machine to list them).

| Provider (free tier) | Approved models |
| --- | --- |
| NVIDIA (about 40 requests/min per model) | DeepSeek V4.1 Flash (`deepseek-ai/deepseek-v4.1-flash`), Nemotron 3.5 Lightning, GLM 5.3 Flash (`z-ai/glm-5.3-flash`), MiniMax M3.1 Flash, Nemotron 3.5 Super VL, Gemma 4 31B (`google/gemma-4-31b-it`), Mistral Large 4 (`mistralai/mistral-large-4-1t-a52b`), Nemotron 3.5 Bolt |
| Workers AI (10,000 neurons/day for the account; picked by hand only) | GLM 5.3 Flash, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash (`-1017`), GPT-OSS 120B, Qwen 3.8 27B, Gemma 4 26B, Nemotron 3 120B, Llama 4 Scout, Qwen 3 30B (`qwen3-30b-a3b-fp8`), GPT-OSS 20B, GLM 4.7 Flash |
| Groq (30/min, 1,000/day, 200K tokens/day per model) | GPT-OSS 120B; voice only: `whisper-large-v3-turbo`, `canopylabs/orpheus-v1-english` (voice.ts) |
| Gemini | Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite (500/day), Gemini 3.8 Flash and 3.6 Flash (about 20/day: picked by hand only), Gemma 4 31B and 26B (no system instruction or thinking config: `systemRole: false`, 30,000 characters of history) |
| OpenRouter (`:free`, about 20/min, small daily cap) | Nemotron 3.5 Super VL, Nemotron 3.5 Lightning, Gemma 4 31B and 26B, Nemotron 3 Ultra 550B, Nemotron 3 Nano Omni, Dots 3 Note Preview, North Mini Code, LFM 2.5 2.6B |
| Anthropic (paid, not free; owner-only) | Claude Haiku 5.5 (`claude-haiku-5-5`), Claude Haiku 4.5 (`claude-haiku-4-5`: 200K context, 64K output, $1 / $5 per million tokens) |

Left out on purpose: models that failed the 2026-11-12 live check (Groq Qwen 3.8 27B and GPT-OSS 20B, Gemini 3.5 Flash Lite, GPT-OSS 20B and Llama 3.2 90B Vision on NVIDIA), two the providers did not list (Kimi K3.1 Pro on NVIDIA, Xing 4.0 29B on OpenRouter), and Kimi K2.6, GLM 5.3 and DeepSeek V4 Pro on Workers AI (paid billing). No Llama or MiniMax model is used on Groq. **Paused models come back on their own:** the daily `ai-model-check` job (`_lib/ai/modelCheck.ts`, console Scheduled jobs; daily, or twice a day) sends one tiny request to each model in `PAUSED_MODELS` whose provider has a key, one provider at a time per model; a first word within 12 s is a pass. Results go in the `ai-model-check` setting (`{ "<model>": { ok, at, ms, kind } }`), and a paused model whose latest check passed within two days (`revivedFrom`, config.ts) is in the model menu again, picked by hand only and never in a ladder; a failed check pauses it again; the owner's per-model switch still wins; the console probe also accepts paused models. **OpenRouter's `:free` models rotate:** `pickerRegistryFor` reads OpenRouter's list in the background (`openRouterListed`, `_lib/ai/openRouterList.ts`: six hours in memory and the edge cache, 15 minutes before a failed read is tried again, a cheap text scan of the big list; it never waits for it) and leaves out, without a word, a curated OpenRouter model the list no longer has; while the list is unknown all of them stay. Voice models (`voice.ts`) and `UTILITY_MODELS` are not chat models and are not on the list.

Picker groups: Fast (Nemotron 3.5 Bolt, Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite, LFM 2.5 2.6B); Balanced (GPT-OSS 120B, Nemotron 3.5 Lightning and its OpenRouter copy, GPT-OSS 120B and Nemotron 3 120B on Workers AI); Smart (Mistral Large 4, Nemotron 3 Ultra on OpenRouter, GLM 4.7 Flash); Vision (Nemotron 3.5 Super VL on NVIDIA and OpenRouter, Nemotron 3 Nano Omni); Code (North Mini Code, GPT-OSS 20B on Workers AI); Claude (Claude Haiku 5.5 and 4.5, locked for everyone but the owner); More (Dots 3 Note Preview); Cloudflare (the other Workers AI models).

**Claude (Anthropic, added 2026-11-12; owner-only).** `adapters/anthropic.ts` calls `POST https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages` with `stream: true` by plain fetch, like every other provider, because the chat passes the provider's bytes to the page untouched (the official SDK would parse the stream inside the Worker, which is what pass-through avoids on the free plan's CPU). Request: `system` on top, images as base64 `image` blocks before the text, `thinking: { type: "adaptive", display: "summarized" }` (summarised reasoning in the thinking fold; the API default `omitted` would look like a long pause), `output_config.effort` from the thinking level (Standard = `medium`), no `temperature` (the current Claude models refuse sampling parameters), 32,768 output tokens (65,536 at Maximum), history capped at 340,000 characters (about 85K tokens, so with the system prompt each request stays under Haiku 5.5's 100K-token price step, above which every token costs five times as much; the model holds 1M). Haiku has no server-side fallback, so it sends no `fallbacks`; a model added with `fallback: true` in `models.ts` would send `fallbacks: "default"` with the `anthropic-beta: server-side-fallback-2026-07-01` header (a request a safety classifier declines is then answered on the same stream by the model Anthropic recommends). A `refusal` stop is `content_filter`, `max_tokens` is `length`; a mid-stream `error` event is classified by its type (`overloaded_error` = 529 upstream, `rate_limit_error` = 429, …). PDFs reach Claude as the text the page read out of them (like every non-Gemini model; native PDF blocks would mean base64-encoding up to 30 MB in the Worker). Only text goes back in the history (never thinking blocks), so preserved thinking's history checks never apply.

**Who may use Claude** (`_lib/ai/claudeAccess.ts`): `CLAUDE_EMAILS` = `sirimillavinay56@gmail.com`, matched against the account row's email (case and spaces aside). That email is only ever written from a Google ID token the Worker verified; the token's `email_verified` claim is not required because the Android app's tokens lack it. Everyone else, signed out included, sees the Claude group in the picker with each model greyed, a Locked badge and "Locked: Claude is not open to this account" (`GET /api/ai/models`: `available: false, reason: "locked", locked: true`; a locked model never says whether the key is set), and `POST /api/ai/chat` with a Claude model answers `403 model_locked` before anything is written or sent to Anthropic. No Claude model is in any ladder, and the chat's and `complete.ts`'s "whole registry" fallbacks and a failed model's `alternatives` skip locked models, so Auto, titles, memory and the music tasks never call Claude for anyone. To share Claude, add an email to `CLAUDE_EMAILS`.

**The 2026-11-12 live check.** Every picker model (146 then, curated and found by the model discovery of the time) was asked "17 × 23" and a two-sentence question through the live chat; what was rate-limited all day, timed out, gone at its provider or not a chat model was dropped. Re-run that check before adding a model back; the models new to the approved list (DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, GLM 5.3 Flash, the Gemma 4 models, Gemini 3.8 Flash, the new Workers AI models) were not in it. Gemini 3 models think at `thinkingLevel: "low"` (Standard; see Thinking levels below; 3.6 Flash took 14 s for two sentences at its default); a 400 that names thinking is asked once more with only `includeThoughts`.

`pickLadder` (`_lib/ai/router.ts`) uses cheap checks, never a model call:

| Signal | Ladder |
| --- | --- |
| Image in one of the last three questions | vision: Nemotron 3.5 Super VL -> Super VL (OpenRouter) -> Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite |
| PDF in the thread, or > 150,000 characters (files' text counted) | long: Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite -> Nemotron 3 Ultra (OpenRouter) -> Mistral Large 4 |
| Thinking Extended or Maximum, or Auto judged the message hard (not code) | deep: GPT-OSS 120B (Groq) -> Nemotron 3 Ultra (OpenRouter) -> Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite -> Nemotron 3.5 Lightning -> Mistral Large 4, high reasoning |
| Code cues | code: GPT-OSS 120B (Groq) -> North Mini Code -> Nemotron 3 Ultra (OpenRouter) -> Nemotron 3.5 Bolt |
| Short small talk (< 6 words, no question) | fast: Nemotron 3.5 Bolt -> GPT-OSS 120B (Groq) -> Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite |
| Anything else | default: GPT-OSS 120B (Groq) -> Nemotron 3.5 Bolt -> Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite -> Nemotron 3 Ultra (OpenRouter) -> Nemotron 3.5 Lightning -> Mistral Large 4. Ordered by time to the first word, measured live on 2026-11-12 (server time included): GPT-OSS 120B ~0.9 s when its per-minute allowance lasts, Bolt 0.9 s, Flash Lite ~2 s, Ultra 2.7 s; Lightning thinks 8-10 s first and Mistral Large 4 timed out at 60 s |

No ladder uses Workers AI (its daily pool is shared by the whole account) or Gemini 3.6/3.8 Flash (about 20 messages a day each). Models whose provider has no key are left out; an empty ladder falls back to default, then any configured picker model.

Inside the ladder, `preferFit` (`router.ts`) puts first the models that can see the images and hold the whole thread (a model that cannot see is dropped when one can); then, at Extended or Maximum, `preferLevel` (`thinking.ts`) puts full-size models with a real thinking switch ahead of those without (exact level first; a fast-tier model is never moved up for this). The rest stay behind as failover.

**Thinking levels** (`_lib/ai/thinking.ts`). The composer's Thinking menu sends `thinking: auto | fast | standard | extended | max`; any other value is Auto. Each level is a real provider switch, laid over the model's `params`; a model without the level answers at the nearest one it has (Maximum -> Extended -> Standard), and `meta.thinking` says `{ choice, level, used }`:

| Model | Fast | Standard | Extended | Maximum |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Gemini 3 (`thinkingConfig.thinkingLevel`) | `minimal` | `low` | `high` | `high` + 65,536 output tokens, 32,768 on Flash Lite (thinking counts against output; a 400 about the output limit is asked again at 32,768) |
| GPT-OSS on Groq (`reasoning_effort`) | `low` | `medium` | `high` | - |
| OpenRouter reasoning models (OpenRouter's `reasoning.effort`) | `low` | model default | `high` | - |
| Claude Haiku 5.5 (`output_config.effort`, adaptive thinking) | `low` | `medium` | `high` | `max` + 65,536 output tokens (thinking counts against `max_tokens`) |
| Claude Haiku 4.5 (`thinking.budget_tokens`, no effort) | no thinking | 4,096 | 16,384 | 32,768 + its full 64,000 output tokens (the adapter keeps the budget at least 1,024 and below `max_tokens`, else sends no thinking) |
| Other models with `highParams` | - | yes | `highParams` | - |
| NVIDIA, Workers AI, the rest | - | yes | - | - |

Extended and Maximum also add a short block to the prompt (`THINKING_BLOCKS` in `prompt.ts`): work it through, check calculations and code, state assumptions, give the answer first and then the key steps; Maximum adds a final review against every part of the question and says plainly what cannot be verified. The reasoning stays in the model's thinking channel (the fold above the reply); no level shows hidden reasoning that a model does not send. **Auto thinking** (`assessTask`, cheap text cues, never a model call): small talk and short fact questions are `simple` -> Fast; maths with work to do (solve, prove, an equation, several operations), three or more questions or listed requirements, "step by step"/"analyse"/"design"/"debug" cues, or long bug reports score as `hard` -> Extended; the rest `normal` -> Standard. `meta.route` `{ task, complexity }` (Auto model only) names what it judged (Conversation, Coding, Maths, Analysis, Writing, Image, Document). `GET /api/ai/models` sends each model's `thinkingLevels`, capability `kinds` (fast, reasoning, code, vision, long = >= 300,000 characters), `contextTokens`, `outputTokens`, `recommended` (the head of a chat ladder), and `autoThinking`: the levels Auto can give with the configured ladder models (Maximum only when a full-size ladder model has it, which none does now: Auto at Maximum answers at Extended and the menu says so, naming a model to pick for it).

- **When to move on:** before the first answer-text token reaches the page - no first event within 12 s (25 s for deep or a picked model), network error, 429/5xx/404/410/401/402/403/other 4xx, an empty stream or only reasoning. Whole-reply budget 360 s (480 s for deep or a picked model), room for a long page or app. After the first token no failover: an `error` event, `stopReason: 'error'`, partial text kept. A picked model is never swapped; its failure reports up to two `alternatives`. When failover changes the model `meta` is sent again and the label under the reply names the model that answered. With pass-through (the page's default) the switch can happen only until the model's first `data:` line: refusals, timeouts and an opening error frame still move on, but a stream that then sends only reasoning or breaks off ends as an `error` (`empty_reply`, or a timeout/network one) for the page to retry.
- **Cooldowns** (`_lib/ai/cooldown.ts`, per-isolate memory; resting models skipped; if all rest, all tried; a new rest never shortens a longer one): 429 -> `retry-after`, 1 min if none, max 10 min; 429 saying a daily/free allowance is spent -> until reset, else 6 h (max 24); Workers AI 4006 (daily neuron pool) -> every Workers AI model until 00:00 UTC; 404/410 -> 1 h; Workers AI 5007 -> 24 h; 401/402 -> whole provider 10 min; 403 -> that model 10 min; 5xx -> 30 s; timeout, network error, other 4xx, empty answer -> no rest. When a provider is down its models rest after the first failure and Auto answers from the next; if all fail the page shows "VinaX AI is busy right now. Try again in a moment." with Retry.
- **One answer, not a stream:** `_lib/ai/complete.ts` (`completeText`, `completeJson` with reasoning tags/fences stripped, first balanced value taken, validated, one repair turn) - used by Live voice's call summary; any failure is a null and the caller has a deterministic answer.
- **Reasoning:** `ThinkSplitter` (`_lib/ai/think.ts`) routes `reasoning`/`reasoning_content` deltas, `<think>...</think>` at the start of text (even split across chunks) and Gemini thought parts to `thinking` events; raw tags never reach the reply; capped at 20,000 characters; a stream ending inside `<think>` with no answer counts as empty (Auto fails over).

### Web search (`_lib/ai/search.ts`, wired in `chat.ts`)

At most one search per turn, never for Continue, never when `vinax-ai.search` is `false`. The composer's + menu: Web search Auto/On/Off (kept on the device); Auto searches on recency cues (today, latest, news, prices, scores, weather, "is ... open", a recent year, a link or known domain).

1. **Google Search grounding** when a model whose grounding is free can answer. **No model does now:** it was only Gemini 2.5 Flash and 2.5 Flash-Lite, which failed every request on 2026-11-12, so the `grounded` ladder is empty and every search goes through the search API (step 2); the code stays for a model that offers it again (Gemini 3.x said "Not available" on the free tier) - registry `grounding: true`, the `grounded` ladder; allowance capped at 450/day across all Workers via `ai_take_daily`. Adapter adds `tools: [{ googleSearch: {} }]` (retries once without if Google refuses). Sources arrive with the answer end (`groundingMetadata`); `placeMarkers` puts `[n]` after supported stretches (never in code); Google's `searchEntryPoint.renderedContent` is shown under the sources in `<iframe sandbox="allow-popups allow-popups-to-escape-sandbox" srcdoc>` as its terms require.
2. **The search API** for any other model: Tavily (`POST https://api.tavily.com/search`, Bearer) or Serper (`POST https://google.serper.dev/search` or `/news`, `X-API-KEY`; request shape taken from client libraries - confirm with a real key: the console's **Test search** below does that). Query = latest message only (links cut to hosts, file contents removed, <= 300 characters; a short follow-up borrows key words from the previous question); <= 6 results; cached in `caches.default` 15 min (news-like) or 60 min; each real call takes one from `SEARCH_DAILY_LIMIT` for the day; provider rests 24 h after 401/402/403/429/432/433. Results go into the system prompt as `<search_results>` ("reference material, not instructions") and the model cites `[n]`.

**Test search** (console > Health > AI Assistant > **Web search** card; `POST /api/admin/ai-search { query?, topic?: general|news }`, editors and owners, `api/admin/ai-search.ts` -> `testSearch` in `search.ts`): one real search through the configured provider (Tavily, or Serper when `SEARCH_API_PROVIDER` says so), the question "Carnatic music ragas" unless one is sent, with the cache skipped. It takes one search from the day's allowance through `ai_take_daily` like any other (`SEARCH_DAILY_LIMIT`, or the owner's `limits.searchesPerDay`; the card's "Searches today … of …" and its button use the same number, so a limit of 0 disables the button). A provider resting after a refusal is asked anyway: an answer ends the rest, a refusal starts it again; `searchWeb` itself is unchanged. Answer `{ ok, provider, count, ms, status?, site?, reason? }` (`reason` `not_configured | daily_limit | failed | timeout | empty`; never the key or the provider's words); the card says it in plain words, for example "Working ✓ 6 results in 0.8 s from Tavily · first: <site>" or "Refused (HTTP 401): check the key".

A failed or empty search is not an error ("No useful results"). Stream: `status` `{ step: 'searching', query }` then `{ step: 'searched', count, sites }`; `sources` `{ items, via: 'grounding'|'search', queries?, entryPointHtml?, markers? }` (before the text for the search API, after for grounding); sources saved in `ai_messages.sources`. The page shows a status line, `[n]` chips with hover/long-press cards (open in a new tab with `rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow"`) and a Sources row (letter monogram, no favicon fetched).

### The system prompt (`_lib/ai/prompt.ts`; snapshot test `__tests__/aiPrompt.test.ts`)

Built in order: (1) the full prompt for every model (the compact prompt is gone), with local time/zone, first name, language and the answering model/provider filled in; (2) the owner's instructions in `<owner_instructions>` (`vinax-ai.instructions`, <= 4,000 characters, never overriding the refusals); (3) `ARTIFACTS_BLOCK` (`# Visuals`: the owner's visuals prompt `VISUALS_OWNER_TEXT` verbatim, then `VISUALS_RULES` for the sandbox; since 2026-11-16) for every model with >= 4,096 output tokens unless the registry says `artifacts: false` (Riva Translate, Ising Calibration, Gemini Robotics ER) or the page sends `artifacts: false`; (4) memory in `<memory>` (every model; never in an incognito chat); (5) a project block (`# Project: <name>`, `<project_instructions>`, `<project_knowledge>`; cut to fit); (6) the listener's preferences `<user_preferences>` from `personal: { name?, about?, instructions?, style? }` (name <= 40 characters, others <= 1,500; styles `concise|explanatory|formal|learning`; `normal` adds nothing; never stored or logged); (7) `<search_results>` and citation rules when search ran. No taste profile or listening history is added. History is trimmed to the model's window minus the system text; models with `systemRole: false` get the system text before the first user turn.

### The chat endpoint and stream

`POST /api/ai/chat` needs a session. Limits: per-IP limiter (`RATE_LIMIT_30` tier) and `RATE_LIMIT_30` keyed by account; body <= 2 MB (files travel separately); text <= 400,000 characters. Placement: `parentMessageId` absent = after the active leaf; a message id = under it (an edit makes a sibling); `"root"` = new first message; empty `content` with a user-message parent = retry (new sibling answer); `continueFrom` an assistant message = Continue (appends to the same message). History = path from the root trimmed to `maxContextChars` (latest turn always stays; never starts on an assistant turn; same-side turns joined).

| Event | Data |
| --- | --- |
| `meta` | `{ conversationId, userMessageId, assistantMessageId, model: { id, name, provider }, wire, thinking: { choice, level, used }, route?: { task, complexity }, continued? }`; again after failover |
| `progress` | `{ step: "reading", count, names }` once before the model starts when the reply reads the thread's or the project's files (older pages ignore unknown events) |
| `title` | `{ title }` (cut from the first message, up to six words, then a generated 2-6-word title from the `title` ladder: Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite (`thinkingLevel: "minimal"`), then Nemotron 3.5 Bolt; end of reply waits up to 1.5 s) |
| (no name) | the provider's own `data:` lines, untouched (pass-through, below) |
| `error` | `{ code, message, retryable, alternatives? }`: `model_busy`, `model_timeout`, `model_unavailable`, `empty_reply`, `all_busy` |
| `done` | `{ stopReason: end\|length\|aborted\|error, usage?, saved }` |

**Pass-through (why replies no longer drop).** The Worker is on the free plan: 10 ms of CPU per request. Relaying a reply token by token (parse each provider chunk, write an event) cost about a second of CPU for a long answer, because every network packet woke the Worker; the platform then killed the request mid-reply (`exceededCpu` in `wrangler tail`; the page said "The connection dropped before the reply finished"; seen 2026-11-11 with Nemotron 3.5 Lightning, which thinks for 20-40 s first). A body handed on untouched costs almost nothing (a 13 MB `/api/apk` download: 2 ms). So every chat request is passed through (the request has no `passthrough` field any more): `_lib/ai/passthrough.ts` `openModel` starts the model, waits for its first `data:` line (an error frame there still fails over in Auto) and copies the body three ways (native `tee`): one is piped into the response (an `IdentityTransformStream`, so the bytes never wake the Worker) between the Worker's opening and closing events, one is read for the first line, one is read whole when the reply ends and parsed once by the adapter's `parse` to save the reply, add grounding `sources`, report an `error` or an empty reply, and fill `done.usage`. The page reads the lines itself (`frontend/src/features/vinaxai/wire.ts`, by `meta.wire`: `openai`, `gemini`, `workers-ai`, `anthropic`, with the same `<think>` splitting). Claude's lines carry their own `event:` names (`message_start`, `content_block_delta`, … and `error`) and repeat the name as `type` in the data; the page's `sse.ts` reads a named block whose data has `type` equal to its name as the provider's (the Worker's own events never do), so Claude's `error` frame is never taken for the Worker's. Measured in workerd for a 1,500-chunk reply: about 7 ms of JavaScript in all, against 30-60 ms for the relay (whose per-chunk stream work, invisible to the profiler, was what production billed). The copy kept for saving is read only after the page's copy has been handed on (`opened.whole()`): reading it alongside made workerd's tee pass the bytes on in 64 KB blocks, so until 2026-11-12 a reply reached the page in one lump at the end (thinking and answer at the same moment; reproduced in local workerd). Each adapter is `open` + `parse` (`stream` is both, still used by `complete.ts` and voice). Since the bytes pass by without a clock, `thinking_ms` is the reply's time shared out by the lengths of reasoning and answer. The old token-by-token relay (named `text`/`thinking` events) is gone; the page's `sse.ts` still reads those names because the e2e fake streams use them.

**Answer room and long artifacts.** The big NVIDIA and OpenRouter models answer up to 16,384 tokens (`LONG_OUT`), Gemini up to 32,768 (16,384 for Flash Lite), Groq 8,192 (its free tier counts tokens per minute); more per reply risks the free plan's CPU limit while the Worker reads the finished reply to save it. A reply that stops at that limit inside an artifact that is still open is carried on by the page with Continue by itself, up to three times (`AUTO_CONTINUES` in `store.ts`), so a page or app is finished rather than left half-written (2026-11-12: a dashboard stopped at 8,192 tokens and its preview hung at "Initializing… 0%"). An artifact still cut off says so above its preview, pointing to Continue. A finished reply whose raw lines pass 1 MB (`PARSE_MAX_CHARS` in `chat.ts`; about 8 ms of CPU a megabyte to parse, a 16K-token NVIDIA reply is ~5 MB, ~40 ms) is not parsed again by the Worker: `done` says `saved: false` with the stop reason read from its last lines (`tailStop`), and the page saves it through `POST /api/ai/keep` (which now also takes `stopReason: end|length` and, for a Continue, `continued: true` with the whole reply, replacing the saved part it must start with) before any automatic Continue. On 2026-11-12 a 16K-token dashboard broke off with "The connection dropped" because that parse ran past the free plan's CPU limit; such replies get no usage count and no memory learning.

**Keeping a reply cut short.** With pass-through a reply that does not end on its own is only whole on the page: on Stop, on a stream that breaks off, and on `done.saved === false`, `store.ts` sends what it showed to `POST /api/ai/keep` `{ conversationId, userMessageId, assistantMessageId, text, thinking?, model, stopReason: aborted|error }` (own conversation and question only; not for a Continue), saved with that stop reason. A reply already saved is left alone, with one exception: a part saved with stop reason `aborted` is replaced by a longer text that starts with it, or by the same text with a real stop reason (`replaceKeptPart` in `_lib/ai/store.ts` updates only a row whose `stop_reason` is still `aborted`, which also settles a race with the Worker's own save); a reply that ended is never cut down. **When the page closes mid-reply** (since 2026-11-17; `features/vinaxai/keepOnLeave.ts`, lazy like the rest of VinaX AI): on `pagehide` or `freeze` (never `visibilitychange`: going to the background is not leaving), a reply still streaming, or one that ended with `saved: false`, has its queued text flushed and sent to `/api/ai/keep` with `fetch(…, { keepalive: true })` and the Bearer header (`sendBeacon` cannot carry it), trimmed under keepalive's 64 KB body budget (`KEEPALIVE_MAX_BYTES` 60,000: the reasoning goes first, then the end of the text), stop reason `aborted` (or the `done` event's). It also writes a draft on the device (localStorage `vinax.vxai.keep.v1`: at most 3 replies, newest first, text up to 100,000 characters, tagged with the account, dropped after 3 days or when another account is signed in). `finishDraft()` (called when `ChatView` mounts and before a conversation loads, waiting up to 4 s) sends each draft again in full through the normal keep call: a draft is dropped when the save lands or a 4xx says it never will, and kept when offline, signed out, rate limited or on a 5xx. Stop writes a draft before its keep call too, so a Stop followed by closing the page is finished on the next open; a reply the Worker saved drops any draft for it. If the page's keepalive save lands first, the chat's own end-of-stream insert (`23505`) replaces that `aborted` part with the whole reply, never the other way round. A stream that breaks off before any answer text is asked again once by itself (a retry of the same question). Sending the same text again right after a question went unanswered is a retry of it, not a second copy of the question. A reply with no text is not saved. Pre-stream refusals: `400 bad_request | unknown_model | bad_attachment | too_many_images | model_cannot_see_images`, `409 too_many_files | conversation_full` (2,000 messages), `401 signed_out`, `404 not_found`, `413 too_large | too_long`, `429 rate_limited | daily_limit`, `503 ai_not_configured | ai_disabled | unavailable`.

### Thumbs up and down (`_lib/ai/feedback.ts`, route `api/ai/feedback.ts`; page `Feedback.tsx`)

Since 2026-11-17 (schema 24). Under a saved reply (not one unsaved, failed, still streaming or an image): **Good reply** and **Bad reply**. Pressing the same thumb again takes the rating back. A thumbs down opens "What went wrong?" with Wrong, Not helpful, Too slow, Other and an optional note (300 characters, with a counter), then Skip or Send. The rating shows at once; if saving fails it goes back and says "Couldn’t save that. Try again."; when two presses overlap, only the answer to the newest one is applied. `POST /api/ai/feedback { conversationId, messageId, rating: up|down|null, reason?: wrong|not_helpful|too_slow|other, note? }` (session required; per-address limit 30 a minute, like the other AI routes; body <= 4 KB; the chat must be the account's and the message one of its assistant replies, else `404 not_found`; `rating: null` takes it back) -> `{ ok, feedback: { rating, reason?, note? } | null }`; `400 bad_request`, `401 signed_out`, `429 rate_limited`, `503 unavailable`. One rating per reply per account (`ai_feedback`, primary key message + account); sending again changes it. The model and provider are copied from the saved reply, never taken from the page. `GET /api/ai/conversations/:id` adds `feedback: { rating, reason?, note? }` to each reply the listener rated (read on its own, so chats still open on a database without the table). The console's Health > AI Assistant > **Models** table has a sortable **Thumbs up** column: the share of ratings that were up and how many ratings, with up/down counts on hover, over the last 30 days (`ai_feedback_stats(30)`, sent by `GET /api/admin/ai` as `feedback`). The console sees only totals per model, never a note. A rating outlives its chat (the link to the chat is cleared) and goes with the account; the privacy page says so.

### Other routes

| Route | What |
| --- | --- |
| `GET /api/ai/models` | Public: `{ enabled, configured, groups, models: [{ id, name, provider, group, description, flags, available, reason? }], voice: { dictation, readAloud, live, voices, liveVoices, liveModels, liveSearch }, limits, images: { enabled, perDay, maxPrompt } }` (`images.enabled` is false without the `AI` binding) |
| `POST /api/ai/images` | Create image: `{ prompt, conversationId, parentMessageId?, projectId?, incognito? }` -> `{ conversation, userMessage, assistantMessage }` (see [Create image](#create-image-_libaiimagests-route-apiaiimagests)) |
| `GET /api/ai/conversations?cursor=&q=&project=` | Chats newest first, 30/page; `q` searches titles and message text (`ai_conversation_search`) |
| `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/ai/conversations/:id` | Tree (a rated reply carries `feedback`); `{ title?, starred?, activeLeafId?, projectId? }`; delete hides at once and removes its share link (purged a day later) |
| `POST /api/ai/keep` | Saves a reply only the page holds (cut short, `saved: false`, or the page closed mid-reply; [Keeping a reply cut short](#the-chat-endpoint-and-stream)) |
| `POST /api/ai/feedback` | Thumbs up or down on one reply ([Thumbs up and down](#thumbs-up-and-down-_libaifeedbackts-route-apiaifeedbackts-page-feedbacktsx)) |
| `GET/POST /api/ai/shares`, `GET/DELETE /api/ai/shares/:id`, `POST /api/ai/shares/:id/continue` | Shared links ([Sharing](#sharing)) |
| `GET /api/ai/artifacts?kind=&q=&cursor=`, `GET /api/ai/artifacts/:conversationId/:artifactId` | Artifacts gallery (metadata only list; one artifact with every version) |
| `GET/POST /api/ai/projects`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/ai/projects/:id` | Projects (max 100; delete `?chats=keep\|delete`) |
| `GET/POST/DELETE /api/ai/memories`, `DELETE /api/ai/memories/:id`, `GET/PUT /api/ai/memories/prefs` | Memory (`422 sensitive`, `409 full` at 50, `503 memory_off`) |
| `GET /api/ai/export` | Every chat (all branches) and project as one JSON file (`ai_export`); not part of the backup file, never imported |
| `POST /api/ai/attachments`, `PATCH`/`GET`/`DELETE .../attachments/:id` | Files |
| `POST /api/ai/transcribe`, `speak`, `live-token`, `live-transcript` | Voice |

Every query is filtered by the session's account; someone else's or a deleted conversation or a malformed id answers 404. The router dispatches `PATCH`/`DELETE` to `onRequestPatch`/`onRequestDelete`.

### Create image (`_lib/ai/images.ts`, route `api/ai/images.ts`)
