The three panels are glass over it: `--vx-panel-fill` (100% at Solid, 65% at the default, never below 50%), a specular top line and a hairline; they do **not** blur (the mesh is already soft, and a blurring pane under a scrolling page would cost every frame). What does blur: the sticky page bar once content scrolls under it (tinted with the page's colour), the phone tab bar, the mini player (in the artwork's colour), the Now playing panel's bar, the profile menu, the search suggestions, sheets, dialogs and menus (`.vx-mat-*`). Settings > Appearance > **Glass effect** (`glassLevel` → `--glass-alpha`) runs from Solid (no mesh, opaque panels: the 1.15 look) to Glass; **Background blur** (`glassBlur`) sets how soft things look through the bars. Text-carrying fills keep their floors (chrome 80%, thick 86%), asserted in `__tests__/glass.test.ts`.

### Dials, selector contract and paint path

`styles/templates/base.css` declares dials on `html[data-template]`: `--tpl-r-art`, `--tpl-r-art-sm`, `--tpl-r-np-art`, `--tpl-r-control`, `--tpl-r-input`, `--tpl-r-row`, `--tpl-r-tile`, `--tpl-r-play`, `--tpl-r-play-sm`, `--tpl-r-deck`, `--tpl-r-mini`, `--tpl-font-body`, `--tpl-title-weight/tracking`, `--tpl-section-weight/tracking`, `--tpl-card-title-weight`, `--tpl-press`, `--tpl-enter`, `--tpl-enter-dur`. A style **may** change inks/ember/tide, body face, radii, title weight/tracking, motion, how navigation/mini player/cards/Now Playing backdrop are shaped, glass vs solid; it **may not** change what a control does, which settings exist, hit areas, focus visibility, or anything under true black/high contrast. The contract (asserted by `templates.test.ts`): a style's file styles only `html[data-template='<id>']`; colour blocks carry `:root` on the html selector (it always matches; it is there for its weight, so the blocks keep their place against page rules); win by specificity, never by order (page styles load later); `html.hc[data-template]:not(#_)` outranks every style; text and accent tiers clear WCAG AA; the pre-paint canvas equals `--ink-900`; light and dark both clear WCAG AA; no `html.amoled` or `[data-accent=…]` rule is left anywhere. Paint path: the inline script in `index.html` reads `vinax.settings.v1` before first paint, resolves the theme, sets `.light`/`.dark`, `data-template` (legacy map, fallback `vinax`) and the window canvas from its own table; `applyThemeClasses` keeps them, `<html>` background and `theme-color` (`TEMPLATE_CANVAS`: `#000000` dark, `#ffffff` light, = `--ink-950`) current.

### Materials

Four frosted materials (`.vx-mat-thin|regular|chrome|thick`: translucent fill, backdrop blur, saturation lift, 1px specular top line, hairline):

| Material | Fill (dark) | Blur | Used for |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `thin` | `ink-950` 62% of glass level | 12px + up to 30px | Large quiet panes: the sidebar |
| `regular` | `ink-850` 50-92% | 16px + up to 34px | Floating controls (defined; Now Playing buttons borrow only its line/hairline) |
| `chrome` | `ink-950` 80-97% | 18px + up to 36px | Fixed/sticky bars content scrolls under: top bar (once content is under it, via an `IntersectionObserver` sentinel), phone tab bar, compact player, player deck (carry the artwork colour faintly via `--mat-tint`) |
| `thick` | `ink-850` 86-98% | 24px + up to 40px | Anything carrying a block of text: sheets, dialogs (`<Sheet>`), song menu, right-click popover, snackbars |

Settings > Glass effect sets `--glass-alpha`, Background blur sets `--glass-blur-boost` (`applyGlassLevel` in `utils/theme.ts`); text tiers are floored (`clamp`) so AA holds over any cover (`__tests__/glass.test.ts`). The look's materials follow the dials (`templates/vinax.css`, see Themes and glass); no `backdrop-filter` or `prefers-reduced-transparency` falls back to `--surface-solid`; phones get a lower blur cap. **Never put a material on a row inside a scrolling list.** Now Playing: two heavily blurred artwork copies drift behind the stage (26 s and 31 s, transform only), frosted round buttons without their own backdrop filter, drift stops under either reduced-motion switch.

**Snackbars** (`components/Toasts.tsx`, `store/toastStore.ts`): `toast(text)` or `toast(text, { image, action, duration, key })`; a solid blue notice (`rgb(32 107 196)`, white text, 1.15), radius 8, >= 48px tall, <= 480px wide, optional 36px thumbnail, at most one action; ~4 s (5 s with an action); at most two at once; same words twice show once; a keyed toast replaces the older one with the same key (liking uses key `like`); hover/focus pauses timers; swipe down 36px or Esc dismisses the newest; always-in-DOM polite live region. **Notifications inbox:** the Home bell opens `NotificationSheet` (thick glass; rows with artwork grouped Today/Yesterday/date, then "From VinaX" with the three newest release notes; Android adds "Mute alerts for 7 days").

### Type, spacing, radii, elevation, controls

One face, Figtree variable (`/fonts/figtree-var.woff2` latin and `figtree-ext-var.woff2` latin-ext by `unicode-range`, weights 300-900, SIL OFL, licence `figtree-OFL.txt` beside the files, declared in `styles/index.css`, metric-matched `Figtree Fallback` on Arial), for titles (`--vx-font-display`, weights 700-900, tight tracking) and body (400/700). 1.15 retired Bricolage Grotesque and Manrope from the app; the owner console still loads its own copies. Scale: display `clamp(2.25rem, 4vw, 3.75rem)`, page `clamp(1.875rem, 3vw, 2.625rem)`, section 1.375rem, card/body .9375rem, meta .8125rem, caption .75rem (`.vx-eyebrow` uppercase .08em); headings track -0.04em/-0.01em at weights 900/700; tabular numerals for times. **Never put a font on `#root`** and no style may lead a stack with a system font (Android theme fonts); pinned by `constants/typography.test.ts`. Spacing `--vx-space-1..16` on a 4px grid; radii as above plus `rounded-card` .75rem, `rounded-sheet` 1.25rem, `rounded-pill`; artist artwork is a circle. Elevation mostly flat (`--vx-deck-shadow`, `--vx-art-shadow`, `--vx-shadow-overlay`). **Hit areas:** visual box 36-40px, touch target >= 44px: `IconButton` (requires `label`, `type="button"` default, `::after` pad -inset-1 for sm / -inset-0.5 for md, lg 48px), rows/links `min-height: 44px` (`min-h-touch`), dock items 56px, `Chip` 36px with a 4px pad, coarse-pointer range inputs 44px and hover-only affordances always visible.

### Focus, overlays, motion, top bar

`:focus-visible` draws a 2px white outline with a 2px offset. Buttons: hover lightens and grows to 1.04, active scales down, disabled 45% opacity. **Overlays:** `<Sheet>` (`components/Sheet.tsx`) is the one shell (bottom sheet on phones, dialog from `sm`): `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, `labelledBy`/`label`, `useFocusTrap`, `useDismissOnBack`, reference-counted scroll lock, `size` (`sm|md|lg|2xl`), `padding`, `layout`, `maxHeight` (92dvh/85dvh), `z` 70 (60 boot, 80 update gate), backdrop closes unless `closeOnBackdrop={false}`; do not pass padding/width/height/radius via `className`. Overlays are portalled to `<body>` (a page's animated wrapper would break `fixed inset-0`); `fade-up` fills `backwards` not `both`; `<Sheet>` stops click propagation (portals bubble React events). The web-only wheel rescue (`features/nav/wheelRescue.ts`, off in the Android app) forwards wheel events landing on injected blockers outside `#root`; anything portalled to `<body>` must have `[role="menu"|"dialog"|"listbox"|"status"|"presentation"]` on/inside its root or set `data-vx-overlay`. **Motion:** one easing `--ease-calm`, durations 140/200/320 ms (`--vx-motion-fast`, `--vx-motion-normal`, `--transition-slow`); no bounce; two switches (OS `prefers-reduced-motion` and in-app Reduce motion -> `html.reduce-motion`); scripted scrolls use `scrollBehavior()` from `utils/motion.ts`, never hard-coded `'smooth'`. **Bars** (`components/TopBar.tsx`). From 768px the **window bar** (`WindowBar`) runs across the top of the window: the mark, a round Home button, the search pill ("What do you want to play?", it opens Search; on Search the page's own field and suggestions are portalled into the pill's slot, `#vx-winbar-search-slot`) with Browse (Discover) at its end, round icon links for Radio, Flow and VinaX AI (`PRIMARY_NAV`, the nav landmark "Main navigation"), then Ideas & bugs, the language button, ⌘K and the avatar. The **page bar** (`TopBar`) is sticky at the top of the scrolling page: back/forward (phones: the mark), then, once the page scrolls, the page's name (`uiStore.pageTitle`, set by `usePageTitle` and `EntityHeader`) and its play button (`uiStore.pagePlay`, set by `PlayFab`) on the page's colour (`--hero-rgb` from `uiStore.pageTone`); right = the page's actions slot (and on phones the account tools); pages add actions with `<TopBarActions>` (portals into `#vx-topbar-actions`; keep to `IconButton`s).

**Hard rules:** no raw hex in components (exceptions: canvas share cards, chart palettes, the canvas table); no white-alpha borders/text (use the ink ramp and glass hairlines); no arbitrary radii; icon-only controls use `IconButton`; touch targets >= 44px; new overlays use `<Sheet>` (custom portals mark `data-vx-overlay`); scripted scrolling uses `scrollBehavior()`; sticky-under-top-bar reads `--vx-topbar-h`; every animation answers both reduced-motion switches; look changes keep `theme.test.ts`, `contrast.test.ts`, `templates.test.ts`, `typography.test.ts`, `glass.test.ts` green; mini player and phone tab bar never show page text through them.

### Adding an app style

1. `frontend/src/utils/theme.ts` - add the id to `TemplateId` and its canvas to `TEMPLATE_CANVAS`.
2. `frontend/index.html` - add the same canvas to the pre-paint table.
3. `frontend/src/constants/templates.ts` - add the `TemplateOption` (`id`, `label`, `tagline`, `traits`, `swatch` {canvas, raised, accent, second}); `templates.test.ts` asserts the count, so change it there too.
4. `frontend/src/styles/templates/<id>.css` - the colour block (with `:root` on the html selector), dials, then only the style's own layout rules.
5. `frontend/src/styles/templates/index.css` - import the file.
6. A second look needs a way to choose it: 2.0 removed the picker (`TemplatePicker`) and its miniatures with the six earlier styles.
7. `npx vitest run src/constants/templates.test.ts src/utils/theme.test.ts src/constants/typography.test.ts src/__tests__/contrast.test.ts` - failures name what was missed.
8. Build, then `node scripts/csp-hashes.mjs` (the inline script changed; `cspHashes.test.ts` fails until `public/_headers` matches).
9. Look at it: every destination, phone and desktop width, high contrast, reduced motion.

## App styles

VinaX has **one look**: `vinax`. 1.15 made it black and green, always dark. The six styles of 15.0-1.13 (Lumen, Verdant, Monsoon, Harbour, Verse, Onyx) and their picker are gone; an id stored in settings or a backup by any earlier release (those six, and `aura`, `pulse`, `sangam`, `nocturne`, `vibe`, `marquee` from 11.0-14.x) maps to `vinax` through `normalizeTemplate`/`LEGACY_TEMPLATES` (`utils/theme.ts`) and the settings sanitiser. The `template` setting, `html[data-template]` and the pre-paint stamp stay, so a second look can still be added (steps above).

| | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Canvas | Dark: window `#000000`, panels and page `#121212`, hover `#1a1a1a`, cards `#1f1f1f`, menus and dialogs `#2a2a2a`; text white, secondary `#b3b3b3`. Light: window `#ffffff`, panels `#f8f8f8`, cards `#ebebeb`, text `#121212`, secondary `#4d4d4d` |
| Accent | Green `#1ed760` (hover `#3fe479`, pressed `#1db954`; light `#148a3c`), black labels on it. Hover states are washes of the text colour, never a colour |
| Shape | Artwork 4px (circles for people), cards and panels 8px, sheets 12px, chips and buttons pills, round play buttons, menus 4px with 2px items |
| Type | Figtree for everything: entity titles 900 at up to 6rem, page titles 800, section titles 700 at 24px, rows 16px/14px at 400 |
| Chrome | Glass (1.15.1): panels over the artwork-coloured window, frosted bars, player and overlays; solid at Glass effect = Solid |
| Motion | `tplFade` 200ms, press 0.96, hover grows 1.04 |

**Layout (768px and wider).** A black window: the window bar across the top (`WindowBar`), then three rounded #121212 panels 8px apart (`.vx-panels` in `AppLayout`): **Your Library** on the left (`Sidebar.tsx`: + to start a playlist, filter chips Playlists/Albums/Artists/Podcasts, find-in-library, a Recents / Alphabetical sort, 64px rows with 48px artwork, the row whose list is playing in green with a speaker; collapses to a 72px rail; tablets always get the rail), the page in the middle, and from 1280px the **Now playing view** on the right (`NowPlayingRail.tsx`: what it is playing from, the artwork, title and like, a lyrics card on the artwork's colour, a Next in queue card; or the queue itself). From 1024px the player bar sits under the panels on the black (`.vx-bottom-chrome` is in the flow there): artwork, title and the like control (⊕, then a green ✓ disc: `LikeGlyph` in `FavButton.tsx`) on the left; shuffle, previous, a white round play, next and repeat over a 4px seek bar (white, green with a knob on hover) in the middle; the Now playing view switch (`settings.nowPlayingView`), lyrics, queue (opens in the right panel on wide screens: `uiStore.railView`), devices, volume and full screen on the right; toggles that are on are green with a dot under them. The full-screen player hides the panels.

**Phones.** No window bar: the page bar carries the mark, the page's actions and the account tools; the page's name fades in once it scrolls. The mini player is a 6px card in the playing artwork's colour with a 2px white progress line along its foot; the tab bar (`DOCK_NAV`: Home, Search, Flow, Library, VinaX AI) is a black wash, its current tab white and bold. Album, playlist and artist pages centre the cover and put the green play at the right end of the action row. Search's field is white with black type.

**Pages.** Entity pages (`EntityHeader.tsx`): a band of the cover's colour (`useArtTone` → `--hero`) from the top of the panel, darkening into the action row, the 232px cover with a deep shadow, the type, a huge title and a meta line; then the green play and bare 32px icons (shuffle, save ⊕/✓, share, ⋯). Track rows have a 4px white-wash hover and a green title on the playing row. Cards (`MediaCard`) carry 12px of padding with a white-wash hover on a computer, and a green play button rises into the artwork's corner. Home opens on a soft wash of the playing artwork's colour, the greeting, the browse chips (`.vxh-topchips`) and Jump back in as a 2-up / 4-up grid of compact tiles.

Rules the look keeps (asserted by `templates.test.ts`, `typography.test.ts`, `contrast.test.ts`): colour blocks carry `:root`; text and accent tiers clear WCAG AA on the canvas and on cards, black labels clear AA on the green; `--ink-900` equals the canvas in `TEMPLATE_CANVAS` and the pre-paint table; high contrast outranks the look; the file styles only its own selector. User-facing copy never names another product (`templates.test.ts` bans the names in style copy).

## Charts, trends and canvas clips

**Charts and trends.** A list reaches a listener labelled as what it is: a **chart** item is a catalogue song matched with confidence to an entry an outside source reported as popular (source, rank, region, observation time, evidence link); an **editorial** entry is labelled editorial with an evidence link and expires; a **catalogue list** is labelled as a catalogue list. The 14.0 schema keeps no trend tables, so `GET /api/trends` always answers `200 { generatedAt, sources: [], items: [] }` (`OPTIONS` 204; cache `public, max-age=60, s-maxage=300`; the `?region=` query is accepted and ignored) and the app does not ask it: the client config carries `trendsConnected: true` only while `TRENDS_CONNECTED` in `backend/worker/functions/_lib/clientConfig.ts` is true (false today; `useAppConfig.ts` `trendsConnected()`, off until the config loads). While it is off the Charts page does not mount "Public charts" (`VerifiedCharts`), `trendSignalNow` answers the empty signal with no request, `services/trends/client` never loads and the recommendations debug panel says "trends: not connected". With it on, the Charts page hides "Public charts" when no chart source is connected (`services/trends/present.ts` `verifiedView`: `unavailable | not_connected | empty | items`). **Most played on VinaX this week:** VinaX's own chart from `/api/charts/vinax` (`features/charts/vinaxChart.ts`, lazy only: `parseChart` drops rows without the expected fields and keeps rank order and `https` images; when 10 or more songs come back the first 20 are looked up with `getSong`, four at a time at low priority, so they play and pass `useShelfSafety` on their full records; cached 3 hours). The Charts page shows its top 10 at the top of the left column, where "Public charts" was, with **Show all** to `/top-songs`, only when at least 10 songs pass `useShelfSafety`. `/top-songs` (`ChartLandingPage`) uses the same chart as its feed, labelled "Most played on VinaX this week", under the same rule, and otherwise falls back to the `top hit songs india <year>` search seed. **Top Charts (1.15.1):** the Charts page leads with the catalogue's chart playlists in a grid (Most Searched Songs, Most Streamed Love Songs, Trending Today, the decade charts) from `/charts` for the listener's languages or one language picked from the chips, then the songs of the Trending Today chart ranked, then the catalogue's trending playlists and albums, new albums and featured playlists. Every catalogue feed asks for at most 24 items (`MAX_FEED_LIMIT` in `services/api/catalog.ts`): the catalogue answers `success:false` above that, which is why the Charts lists (and Home's Most Searched pick) were empty before. The recommender's `verified-trend` source therefore gets no candidates. Search's trending chips and the `/most-searched` chips come only from the catalogue's top searches (`getTopSearches`); when that fails or is empty no chips show. `/api/trending-searches` (answers `{ queries: [] }`) is no longer called and stays for older installed apps. Kept code: `backend/worker/functions/api/trends.ts`, `_lib/trends/types.ts` (`RawTrendItem`), `matcher.ts` (+ test), `catalog.ts`, `frontend/src/services/trends/`; turning trends back on means setting `TRENDS_CONNECTED` once a pipeline feeds the route. The pipeline of 7.2-13.9.1 (six-hourly ingest workflow, provider adapters, five `vinax_trend_*` tables, review queue) no longer exists. Bringing it back needs a snapshot/observation store with unique keys per source/region/chart/time, a provider registry whose terms allow rank metadata with attribution, the ingest loop with a quota ceiling and retention, and the read shape the app already understands. Tests: `npx vitest run worker/functions/api/trends.test.ts worker/functions/_lib/trends` (backend), `npx vitest run src/services/trends` (frontend).

**Canvas clips (Now Playing video).** The canvas (`frontend/src/components/SongCanvas.tsx`) auto-fits every clip: a clip close to the screen shape fills it (`object-cover`); a mismatched one (past 1.45x overscan) plays whole (`object-contain`) over a blurred, zoomed copy of itself, with the blurred album artwork as fallback while loading; re-decided per clip and on rotation. The music-video source publishes preview clips as `..._v_<height>p_p.mp4`; probed October 2026 every clip has 540p (the catalogue's `previewUrl`) and 360p; 720p/1080p/1440p/2160p answer 404 and the full-length `streamUrl` is not served, so the canvas is 540p at best and the player says so rather than printing "HD". The ladder (`services/canvas/quality.ts`) probes heights above 540p once a day while none exists, then per clip; Full HD / 4K switch on by themselves when the source adds them; **Auto** plays the highest rendition not exceeding the screen's longer side in device pixels; the quality chip pins a height; a failing rendition steps down. Full-screen view (the button while a clip plays): tap once for controls, double-tap anywhere likes (never unlikes) with a heart where the finger landed, quality chip, full-screen toggle; Esc/Android back/chevron return. Content spec for clips made for VinaX: mobile 9:16, 1080x1920 recommended (720x1280 minimum), 3-8 s seamless loop, MP4 (or JPG still); desktop/TV 16:9, 1080p+, H.264 safe floor (HEVC varies); no audio track (muted); under ~4 MB; `-movflags +faststart`. Example: `ffmpeg -i source.mp4 -t 8 -an -vf "scale=1080:1920:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop=1080:1920" -c:v libx264 -preset slow -crf 23 -movflags +faststart canvas-9x16.mp4`.

## Data and privacy

Written from the code: the storage registry (`frontend/src/constants/storage-keys.ts`), `frontend/src/features/settings/backup.ts`, the network clients under `frontend/src/services/` and the Worker routes. The in-app text is `frontend/src/pages/PrivacyPage.tsx`; any change to what is collected (or adding advertising) must update that page and this section in the same change. The gating code is `services/analytics/telemetry.ts`, `recTelemetry.ts`, `sessionInsights.ts` (usage-sharing choice).

### The short version

- **Device first, account second.** Library, history, settings and the taste profile live on the device; a signed-in listener's also sync to the account as one copy.
- **Features that need the network send what they need.** Browsing sends search words and ids to the catalogue source. Listening history and the taste profile stay on the device (a signed-in listener's also sync to the account as one copy); the heard-set never leaves it. Nothing about listening goes to an AI service; what a signed-in listener types into VinaX AI goes to its providers.
- **Usage statistics are on by default, off with one switch** (14.0.3: the welcome box is ticked and says so; Settings > Region & privacy). While off, no usage events are sent. **Session insights** (third-party, masked replays) are opt-in, off by default.
- **There is no advertising.** No page, web or Android, loads an advertising script; the CSP allows no advertising hosts (the AdSense slot, ownership tag and `/ads.txt` were removed).
- **The server keeps what its features need, in one database, and nothing more:** no precise location; an IP address only in the 90-day `sign_ins` history; usage events purged after a bounded time; rate limiting keys on a hashed network address.

### Your Google account

VinaX needs an account (Google, or email and password): until a listener signs in, every personal page redirects to /login (Privacy, Terms, Contact stay readable; on the website Discover/Charts/Search and entity pages can be browsed first, and a shared video link `/flow?v=<id>` opens Flow signed out; a plain `/flow` still needs an account). Signing in sends Google's ID token to the Worker, which verifies it and stores one `accounts` row: Google account ID, email (and whether verified), name, picture address, linked device (`listener_id` when the device proves its signed ID), time, coarse sign-in facts (edge `country/region/city`, `platform`, `app_version`; not the IP). **Account emails** (with `RESEND_API_KEY`): welcome and sign-in alerts list time, browser/system, the connection's IP (`cf-connecting-ip`) and city/region/country; Resend receives the email address, first name and the email's text; console admins also get sign-in and invite emails; no marketing emails. **Sign-in history (schema 12):** every Google sign-in writes a `sign_ins` row (account, time, IP when it looks like IPv4/IPv6, browser/system, platform, app version, geo, `signup`, `new_device`); the only table with an IP; deleted after 90 days by housekeeping and with the account; the console shows the latest 100 on a profile. **What syncs:** `account_backups` holds one copy in the backup-file format (settings, library/playlists with their share links, smart collections, history, taste profile, bookmarks, Home layout; <= 2 MB; not the heard-set; searches and device identity never sync); merged into each device and updated a few seconds after a change; a version number makes two devices merge. **Session and deletion:** the session is sent only to `/api/auth/*`, `/api/account/*`, `/api/personal/*`, `/api/ai/*`, `/api/profile`, `/api/follow`, `/api/shared-playlist`; **Sign out** forgets it on the device; **Delete account** (Settings > Profile) removes the account row and with it the synced copy and everything cascading. On the web the sign-in button loads from Google; the app uses the system Google sheet. **Email and password (schema 20):** an email account stores the email and the name given, if any; only a bcrypt hash of a password is kept (`account_passwords`), never the password; a 6-digit email code is stored as a keyed SHA-256 hash with the address (`email_codes`), never the code, stops working once used, after five wrong tries or after 15 minutes, and is then deleted (expired ones by housekeeping); deleting the account deletes the hash. The Privacy page says the same, and `ai-catalog.json` lists sign-in `methods: ["google", "email_password"]`.

### What is stored on the device

| Where | What |
| --- | --- |
| `localStorage` registry keys (`vinax.*`; `constants/storage-keys.ts`) | Settings (`vinax.settings.v1`, includes the app style as `template`), player/queue, library, history (last 150), search state, taste profile + Kid-mode profile, region, welcome-done flag, last-seen version, name, the signed-in account's username, alarm, lyric offsets, karaoke history, weekly mix (`vinax.weekly.v1`, `{ v: 2, week, langs, songs }`), playlist share links (`vinax.shared-playlists.v1`) and their device-only invite tokens (`vinax.shared-playlist-tokens.v1`), blend records (`vinax.blends.v1`, device only), output choice, downloads index, room host keys, update reminders, install id `vinax.device-id`, signed id `vinax.signed-device-id`, usage choice `vinax.analytics-consent` |
| `localStorage` feature keys | Bookmarks, smart collections, search workspace, sidebar groups, Home layout, Home signals (`vinax.home.signals.v1`), recommendation memories (`vinax.recs.outcomes.v1`, `seedmemo.v1`, `exposure.v1`, `snoozed.v1`), plays cleared from History that Your VinaX leaves out (`vinax.stats.forgotten.v1`), dismissed banners, VinaX AI prefs (`vinax.vxai.prefs.v1`) and replies kept when the page closed mid-reply (`vinax.vxai.keep.v1`, at most 3, 3 days, this account only), loudness per song (`vinax.loudness.v1`, last 500 songs), podcast episode places and played marks (`vinax.podcast.progress.v1`, at most 50) |
| `sessionStorage` | Boot/chunk-reload counters, restore Undo snapshot (`vinax.backup.undo.v1`), session Home order, session intent, Your VinaX's range (`vinax.stats.range.v1`), the Queue page's Played section open or folded (`vx-queue-played-open`), live Listen Together session (`vinax.together.session.v1`) and the ids of the chat lines this tab sent (`vinax.together.sent.v1`) |
| IndexedDB `tarang-db` | The listen-event log behind taste insights and Your VinaX's stats |
| IndexedDB `vinax-heard` | The lifetime heard-set (canonical keys; never leaves the device except inside an exported backup) |
| Cache storage `vinax-shell-*`, `vinax-audio-v1` | App shell/build assets; downloaded songs: Android's copies and songs saved in the browser (not cleared by SW update or boot recovery; Safari may clear a site's storage after about a week without a visit) |
| Android app storage | Downloaded audio files |

The app style is a display preference only: no request builder or usage event reads it. On first launch after the old AI features were removed, `services/storage/earlyMigrations.ts` erases leftovers (`vinax_ai_chats_v1`, `vinax.ai.*`, `vinax.aiplaylist.*`, AI caches, the `vinax-embeddings` IndexedDB); since 1.14 also the music AI's and personal engine's keys (`vinax.generated.v1`, `vinax.home.ai.v1`, `vinax.home-ai.titles.v1`, `vinax.personal-queue.v1`) and settings (`aiRerank`, `aiDjPicks`, `genInQueue`, `personalEngine`). A generated song a listener saved before 1.14 still plays from the device and keeps its "AI generated" label.

### What leaves the device

**Always-on functional requests:** browsing/searching/opening pages -> the catalogue source (directly or via the Worker's catalogue routes: search words, ids, language, page parameters); playing -> the audio host in the stream URL; artwork -> the artwork host (the CSP allows `https:` images, `data:`, `blob:`); lyrics -> an open lyrics database then the catalogue (title, artist, duration; lyric-word search sends the words); app config/flags/announcements/blocklist/site mode/version/experiments/trending searches -> the Worker (nothing personal); `/api/trends` (region code/language/limit); Charts and `/top-songs` -> `/api/charts/vinax` (nothing personal); `/api/geo` (nothing beyond the request; asked only while region inference is allowed and no override, at most every 12 hours); `/api/username` (username, display name, install id, signed id, current username when changing; availability checks send only the candidate); `/api/feedback` (message, type, platform, version, display name, install id; the Worker adds country/city); `/api/push/*` (web: endpoint, keys, app language, time-zone offset; Android: token, platform, language, offset; the Worker adds edge geo; unsubscribing marks the device inactive); Listen Together `/api/room` (room code, display name, playing song, upcoming songs, position; guests heartbeat with the install id and send a leave beacon; a song request carries id/title/artwork/name; reactions one emoji; only the host's key unlocks member names; ending deletes the room and members; idle 6 h swept hourly; while in a room the device also holds one WebSocket to Supabase Realtime, the room's live line, over which pass the host's song stub and position, chat lines, reactions, the display name and "typing": relayed to the room's other devices, not stored, closed on leaving); `/api/handoff` (ciphertext only, <= 10 minutes, deleted on first read); casting loads a cast script from its vendor only when Connect to a device first opens (web); in the Android app the cast framework (Play services) starts only when Connect to a device first opens; while casting, the TV fetches the stream and artwork URLs itself; saving a song in the browser fetches it from the audio host, as playing it does (no cookies sent, nothing through the Worker). The install id is created the first time something needs it (claiming a username, joining/hosting a room, sending a usage event).


**AI:** nothing about listening is sent to an AI service; Up Next, Home, playlists and lyrics are worked out on the device. Voice search and Drive-mode commands use the browser's/phone's own speech recognition; VinaX never receives audio.

**VinaX AI** (only when a signed-in listener uses it): what they type goes from the Worker to the answering provider (the privacy page names them); the chat never sees listening history apart from memory notes; Personalisation prefs are sent with each message for that reply only (not stored with the chat or logged); web search sends the search words (latest message, links cut to sites) to Google (grounding) or Tavily/Serper, results cached at the edge up to an hour keyed by a hash of the query; conversations are stored with the account (`ai_conversations`, `ai_messages`); a deleted chat is hidden at once and removed within a day; files are stored in a private bucket only the Worker can read (images shrunk on the device, PDF text read on the device), sent to the answering provider, removed with their chat/account, never-sent files after a day; the Worker logs only route, model, provider, latency, status, token counts. **Create image** (when the owner has it on) sends the description from the Worker to Cloudflare Workers AI (FLUX.1 schnell); the picture is stored in the same private bucket as files and goes with its chat or account; the prompt is never logged. Voice only on button press: **Dictation** sends the clip Worker -> Groq (Whisper) or Google (Gemini); **Read aloud** sends the reply words to Groq (Orpheus) or Google; **Live voice** streams the mic straight to Google (Gemini Live) with a one-use token (what both sides say is saved as ordinary messages when it closes, and a call with two or more questions is sent once more, as text, to a quick VinaX AI model to write the short call summary saved after it; while the listener shares their camera, their screen or a photo in a call, those pictures go to Google (Gemini Live) too, about one a second, and are not stored; audio is not stored; with search on, Google may turn speech into searches inside its session; on a search toggle the page sends the latest call turns <= 4,000 characters to put in the new session's instructions, neither stored nor logged). Music pauses while the mic is open. **Memory:** up to 50 notes of <= 200 characters (`ai_memories`; origin, source chat until purged, times) and two switches (`ai_memory_prefs`) plus the incognito flag; learning sends a small model the current notes and the last <= 3 user messages (<= 2,000 characters each) with replies (<= 1,500 each, code/artifacts left out) - nothing else; the model is told never to keep secrets, contact/financial details, health, sex life, religion, politics, caste/ethnicity or anything about others, and the Worker refuses anything that looks like them; notes go to the provider writing each new reply; only the Worker reads them; the console shows only totals; kept until deleted (Delete / Forget everything / account deletion); the owner can turn memory off for everyone. **Shared chats** exist only when the listener presses Create link: a snapshot (`ai_shares`: title, per turn text/cited sources/time; files by name and type only; no reasoning, model, tokens, name, email or picture); anyone with the link (128-bit id, noindex) can read; the owner's account id is never sent; kept until Stop sharing, chat deletion (at once), or account deletion; browsers may keep a loaded copy up to a minute. **Projects** exist only when made: name, description, instructions (<= 8,000), starred, times, files (same private bucket; text read out of text files/PDFs; <= 20 files and 200,000 characters), each chat records its project; project name, instructions and files go to the answering provider with every message in a project chat (a PDF may go to Google's file service up to 48 hours), not copied into messages, not logged, never in a shared link; **Delete project** removes instructions and files at once, chats stay unless "Delete them too". Settings > Your data > **Export VinaX AI chats** downloads every chat (all versions) and every project as one JSON (`GET /api/ai/export`), separate from the backup.

**Only with usage sharing on:** `/api/events` -> `events` + `listeners`: install id, signed id, display name when set, event type, platform, app version; for song events the id, title, first artist, language, artwork URL; types register, open, play, pause, heartbeat, skip, complete, favourite, dislike, playlist add, share, download, lyric miss, error (short message; a playback error also names the song), web vitals, search (words <= 80 characters + result count), presence; the Worker never stores the install id (a server-derived device id, `_lib/identity.ts`) and adds edge country/region/city; events without `x-vinax-consent` are dropped; a `play` is sent only when it counts (>= 5 s heard or 70% of a shorter song, once per run). `rec_served`/`rec_outcome` carry no song. The "Most played on VinaX this week" chart (Charts, Top songs) adds up the `play` events from signed-in listeners' devices over 7 days; a song appears only once at least 5 different accounts played it, and the chart shows only the song, its rank and artwork, never who played it or how many times (the Privacy page says the same). Session insights send layout, taps and scrolls to a third party with on-screen text masked on the device (production only; advertising storage denied).

**The usage choice:** asked on the welcome sheet's languages step (checkbox starts ticked, `useState(true)` in `OnboardingSheet.tsx`); stored as `vinax.analytics-consent` (absent = on; `vinax.usage-sharing-default-on.v1` marks the one-time switch of pre-14.0.3 devices; session insights `vinax.session-insights-consent.v1`, absent = off); enforced by `consented()` on every send plus the `x-vinax-consent` header (the Worker ignores events without it); changeable at Settings > Region & Privacy > Share anonymous usage (off at once) and Session insights (off after next reload); backups do not carry it, device transfer does; rows already sent stay until the retention purge or a deletion.

### Server-side records

| Table | Contents | Removed when |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `listeners` | Device that shared usage or claimed a handle: server-derived device id, display name, handle, platform, version, OS/browser/device, edge geo, now playing, first/last seen | Owner deletes the person |
| `accounts`, `sign_ins`, `account_backups` | Signed-in listeners; sign-in history (IP); synced copy | Delete account / owner delete; `sign_ins` also after 90 days |
| `ai_projects`, `ai_shares`, `ai_memories`, `ai_memory_prefs`, `ai_conversations`, `ai_messages`, `ai_attachments` | VinaX AI data | Their delete actions, or with the account |
| `ai_feedback` | A listener's thumbs up or down on a VinaX AI reply (reason and note on a thumbs down, the reply's model and provider); the console shows only totals per model | Pressing the same thumb again, or with the account; a deleted chat leaves the rating without its chat link |
| `events` | Usage events (with sharing on) | `retention.eventsDays` (default 180), owner purge, or with the person |
| `feedback` | Message, type, name, device id, platform, version, geo, status, owner note | Owner deletes; a deleted person's feedback is detached |
| `profiles`, `follows`, `shared_playlists` | Public profile, follow edges, shared playlists; with **Let people I follow join my room** on, the code of the room you host (shown only to mutual followers while the room is live) | Remove my profile / account deletion; the room code is cleared when the room ends or is swept, when the switch goes off, or by housekeeping once the room is idle 15 minutes |
| `push_devices` | Endpoint+keys or token, platform, language, offset, geo, active flag, last pushed | Marked inactive on unsubscribe or after 180 days; not deleted by those paths |
| `rooms`, `room_members` | Room state; member rows | Host ends; leave; idle 6 h / 1 day by housekeeping |
| `notifications`, `outreach` | What was sent (title, body, link, audience filter, counts); per-person automatic push records | Owner retracts (kept, marked); outreach 180 days |
| `settings`, `experiments`, `audit_log`, `uptime_*`, `job_runs`, `blocked_content`, `meta`, `admins`, `owner_notes`, `error_status` | Owner/operations data | Nothing about listeners (owner notes cascade with the account) |
| `HANDOFF` KV | IndexNow URL list (`indexnow:urls`); Android push setup reports (`fcm-reports`: stage, result, app version, device string; newest 30); the device-transfer relay is retired | URL list replaced by each run; reports 30 days after the last one |
| Rate limiting | Counters keyed by route + hashed address (peppered) | Short-lived; raw address not in the key |

Deletion paths: the listener deletes the account (Settings > Profile -> `accounts`, `account_backups`, VinaX AI chats/projects/shares/memory, and any `personal_*` rows left from before 1.14); the owner deletes a person in the console (device row, usage events, room memberships, account and backup; feedback detached; reason in the audit trail); Database Maintenance can purge usage events older than a chosen number of days for everyone. A local erase removes nothing on the server.

### The backup file

Settings > Your Data exports one JSON: `{ "format": "vinax-backup", "schemaVersion": 2, "app": "vinax", "appVersion", "exportedAt", "categories": { "<category id>": { "<storage key>": "<value>" } } }`. Categories: `settings` (`vinax.settings.v1` incl. app style; inferred region dropped) + region key, `library` (favourites, playlists with tags/pins/descriptions, Listen Later, saved albums/artists, hidden songs/artists, and `vinax.shared-playlists.v1`, the share links of shared playlists: local playlist -> `/p/` copy, version, public, never the invite token; optional, so older files still load; a merge unions the links with `mergeShareMaps`, the summary adds "· N shared"), `smartCollections`, `history` (last 150), `taste` (profile + Kid-mode profile), `searches`, `bookmarks`, `homeLayout` (order, hidden, headline), `identity` (display name, welcome-done flag; a username in an older file is ignored, since the username comes with the account), `extras` (alarm, lyric offsets, karaoke history, sidebar groups), `heard` (<= 200,000 canonical keys; restore only adds; never in account sync). Never included (`BACKUP_EXCLUSIONS`): downloaded audio/paths, install id/signed id, Listen Together host keys, the usage-sharing choice, queue/position/caches, update reminders/What's New read state, VinaX AI chats (an older file's `aiChats` is ignored with a warning). `parseBackup()` refuses > 8 MB, non-JSON, non-VinaX files and a newer `schemaVersion`; each category is sanitised on its own (settings filtered to known keys of the right type; unknown app style falls back to the default). **Restore** (Backup Center: shows each category next to the device's; choose categories; **Replace** makes chosen categories exactly the file's - in `identity` a missing value never undoes the welcome flow or name; **Merge** unions: library lists by id, same play once, taste profile of the side that learned more kept): snapshot of touched keys to `sessionStorage` (`vinax.backup.undo.v1`), one all-or-nothing batch writer, store writes frozen then reload, **Undo that restore** until the tab closes; optional "Download a safety copy first"; quick restore = replace of everything. **A new device:** signing in with the same account brings the synced copy across; the backup file is the offline fallback. Move to a new device (the encrypted one-time transfer) is gone: `/handoff` redirects to Settings > Your data and `/api/handoff` answers `410 gone`. **Erasing:** Clear history/favorites (Undo; clearing history also notes the cleared stretch in `vinax.stats.forgotten.v1` so Your VinaX leaves those plays out of the listen-event log), `clearPersonalization` (taste profile, recommendation memories, Home signals; favourites stay), Reset app state > Erase everything (listen-event log, `vinax-heard`, `vinax-embeddings`, every `localStorage` key starting `vinax`, then reload; does not clear the downloaded-audio cache or `sessionStorage`).

## SEO and indexing

- **Static pages:** the build writes prerendered routes, the 72 mood x language hubs, song/album/artist pages and every sitemap (`sitemap.xml`, `sitemap-static.xml`, `sitemap-hubs.xml`, `entity-pages.xml`) as static files (Worker routes were removed to protect the daily request quota; retired maps 301 to the index in `_redirects`). Only built pages answer 200 under `/song/`, `/album/`, `/artist/`, `/playlist/`, `/lyrics/`; any other URL there gets that folder's `404.html` (plain app shell with `noindex`, written by `frontend/scripts/prerender.mjs`) with status 404 - listeners still get the app (the page renders from the id in the URL), crawlers no longer see a copy of Home with Home's canonical. Built pages link only to other built pages; after the main list the build adds the pages they link to most (`ENTITY_MAX_LINKED`, default 1500). Charts, Top Songs, Trending and language pages carry song lists in crawlable text.
- **Explore and Regions folded in (1.16):** `/explore` and `/regions` are no longer pages: they left `scripts/prerender.mjs` ROUTES and NAV_LINKS (two fewer static routes) and `sitemap-static.xml.ts`. Because both had been prerendered with their own canonical, `frontend/public/_redirects` 301s `/explore`, `/explore/*` to `/discover/` and `/regions`, `/regions/*` to `/languages/`, and `/festivals` now 301s straight to `/discover/` (no chain); the router redirects too. `/discover` and `/languages` were already prerendered, in the sitemap and browsable signed out; `/explore` and `/regions` stay in `AuthGate`'s `BROWSE_PATHS` so old links reach the redirect signed out. `llms.txt` and `ai-catalog.json` no longer mention them.
- **Catalogue sources:** the build reads VinaX's own catalogue API (`musicapi.vinaxmusic.online`) first and public mirrors after; a host build writing fewer than 300 entity pages (`ENTITY_MIN_PAGES`) fails so Pages keeps the last good deployment (on 2026-11-05 mirrors answered 429/402 and a build went live with no entity pages at all).
- **Daily SEO pages** (`.github/workflows/seo-pages.yml`, 02:00 India time or by hand): grows song/album/artist pages by up to 3,000 a night until 18,800 (`ENTITY_MAX_TOTAL`; the Pages Free plan allows 20,000 files per site and the app is ~610, so going further needs the paid plan's 100,000-file limit); keeps a page store (`frontend/.seo-corpus`, carried in the Actions cache), rebuilds the charts' pages, re-renders the 1,000 oldest pages, adds the pages Google already knows but the site lacks (`frontend/scripts/seo-wanted.txt`, from Search Console's Page indexing report; refresh it by hand), then the pages the others link to most, deploys with `wrangler pages deploy`; spends no Worker requests. Every build (including the host's own) first downloads the pages the live sitemap lists that it lacks, so no build shrinks the site; one that would drop > 20% of live pages fails (`ENTITY_MIN_PAGES=0` overrides). Needs `CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN` including Account > Cloudflare Pages > Edit; without it the job does nothing.
- **robots.txt** stays a static file on every host (a Worker-served robots.txt answered 429 when the quota ran out and Google stopped crawling); the bots card in the console writes the `robots` setting and triggers a rebuild via `PAGES_DEPLOY_HOOK`; `GET /api/robots` builds the text; `status.vinaxmusic.online` and `vinax.pages.dev` answer `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`; shared AI chats are `noindex, nofollow`.
- **Browse pages and machine-readable catalogue (1.12):** New Releases, Top Playlists, Top Artists, Podcasts, Radio and Videos are prerendered (`scripts/prerender.mjs` ROUTES, with CollectionPage JSON-LD) and listed in `sitemap-static.xml.ts`; `public/llms.txt` describes the app for assistants; `public/.well-known/ai-catalog.json` (also `/ai-catalog.json`) lists pages, URL patterns, actions, pricing and sign-in facts and is excluded from Functions in `_routes.json`. Link previews use `/icons/icon-512.png` (the old `og.png` banner is gone).
- **IndexNow:** the Worker's `indexnow` job (every 6 h) posts new sitemap URLs to Bing/Yandex/Seznam/Naver (Google reads the sitemap); key file `frontend/public/<key>.txt`.
- **Baseline (2026-11-05):** 0 indexed pages at the time; host redirects are Cloudflare Redirect Rules, not `_redirects`; dashed song ids (`<slug>-<id>`).
- **Owner checklist (code cannot do these):** verify the domain in each search engine's webmaster console and submit `https://www.vinaxmusic.online/sitemap.xml`; after a few days review non-indexed URLs; paste a song link and the Home link into messaging apps - each should show a title and artwork card; weekly read the console Dashboard and Growth & SEO pages, Analytics and the Feedback inbox. Lighthouse (`lighthouse.yml`) fails on accessibility and search checks for prerendered routes.

## Legal and copyright

A plain-language summary for maintainers, not legal advice. Listener-facing pages: `/terms`, `/privacy`, `/dmca`, `/contact`. The repository carries no licence file.

| Topic | Position |
| --- | --- |
| Nature of the service | A free music player that streams music, artwork and lyrics from public catalogue sources. Hosts no media files, charges nothing, sells no data |
| Copyright | All songs, recordings, compositions, artwork and lyrics remain the property of their artists, composers, labels and publishers; attribution (title, artists, album, film) is shown as the catalogue provides it |
| Takedowns | Rights holders ask through the in-app DMCA page (`/dmca`) or contact page (`/contact`); a verified request is honoured by adding the content to the server-side blocklist (`/api/blocklist`, console Blocked Songs & Albums), which every client reads |
| Advertising | None on the website or the Android app |
| Listener data | Listeners sign in with Google; favourites, history and taste profile are stored on the device with export and erase, and sync to the account; server-side usage statistics depend on the usage-sharing choice. [Data and privacy](#data-and-privacy) is the authority |
| Outside services, by kind | A public music catalogue and its media CDN; an open lyrics database; static hosting and an edge Worker platform; a push service; a hosted database; AI model providers for VinaX AI only (only through the Worker). Operational names are in the deployment and operations sections and `backend/.env.example` |
| Disclaimer | Provided "as is" without warranty; catalogue availability depends on outside sources and can change without notice |

## Real-device QA script

What automated tests cannot prove. Part 1 is a 22-minute pass on one phone before every release; Part 2 is the longer pass before tagging an Android release and after any change to the audio engine, media session, native files or service worker. Devices: **A** the Android app (newest package), **B** a phone browser on production, **C** a desktop browser, **D** a TV browser. Mark pass/fail; for a fail write device, app style, theme, step and exactly what you saw. Use the narrowest phone available; steps marked **360** can also be run in a desktop device mode at 360 px.

**Part 1 (A or B)**

1. *Welcome (5 min; fresh install or private window):* the sheet shows "Step n of N"; Back works after the first step, optional steps offer Skip; step 1 takes your name and offers "Have a VinaX backup file? Import a file"; the languages step shows the usage-sharing choice (ticked by default); tap songs you like, pass the slides, finish - Home paints with artwork; **360** nothing scrolls sideways or clips a button; reopen - the welcome does not return.
2. *The look (10 min):* start a song first. **1440 and 1280:** the window bar, Your Library, the page and the Now playing view are separate rounded panels on black; the player bar sits under them; the panel switch and the queue button open and close the right panel; Search's field is in the window bar. **1024 and 768:** the library is a rail, the right panel is gone, the queue button opens the Queue page. **390 / 360:** the tab bar is fully visible, labels readable, current tab white; the mini player sits clear of the tab bar and its play/progress work; Now Playing artwork, title, seek bar and controls fit and **Open queue** is reachable (not off the edge); scroll a long page (Home, Settings) - page text does not show through the top bar or the tab bar. Turn on High contrast: text brightens and the focus ring stays visible.
3. *Reduced motion (3 min):* turn on Reduce motion (then repeat with the system setting): page changes are immediate with no cross-fade; Now Playing and queue do not slide or spring.
4. *Playback outside the app (4 min):* lock the phone - the lock screen shows artwork, title, artist, play/pause, next, previous and a seek bar; headset buttons work; switching output (wired <-> wireless) continues from the same position.

**Part 2 (full pass)**

5. *Boot and offline (A; B in Safari on an iPhone, from the Home Screen):* reopen three times (no freeze/crash); download three songs; airplane mode - the app opens on Downloads, each plays, seeking works, one runs into the next; Library, Settings, Help open offline without reload loops; downloading a new song says there is no internet; back online **Download all** on a 20+ song playlist finishes and failures name their reason; airplane mode, swipe closed and reopen twice - opens both times and downloads still play. B: song ⋯ > **Download** saves (Library > Downloads shows its exact size, the space used and free, **Remove all**); outside the Home Screen the Add to Home Screen tip shows; offline, the Home Screen app opens on Downloads and the song plays.
6. *Player and queue (A, B, C):* tap a song - Up Next fills in its language; Now Playing > Up next > Tune this queue (e.g. Devotional) rebuilds Up Next at once; Queue page > Tune this queue rebuilds (played and current songs stay); a song added from the menu sits ahead of automatic picks and stays after another Tune; double-tap the artwork edges - 10 s back/forward; with "Build every queue" more songs arrive before the last ends; take a phone call and hang up - note whether playback resumes by itself; A only: the player widget shows the current song and its buttons work.
7. *Notifications (A, B, C):* B, C: Home's notification card **Turn on**; in the console send a test notification targeting a song - it arrives and tapping opens that song; A: with the app swiped closed send another (needs [Android push setup](#android-push-setup)).
8. *Updates (A):* with an older package the update dialog appears; **Update later** keeps it away for that build while the Settings check still finds it; install - the app reopens on the new version with library, history, settings and style intact.
9. *Backup and restore (A, B):* export a backup; remove a favourite and switch style; Backup Center - choose the file, preview, restore with Merge then with Replace (data matches the preview; after Replace the backup's style is back); **Undo that restore** returns the earlier state; a damaged or unrelated file is reported and changes nothing.
10. *Listen Together (two devices):* start a room and join by code or link - both hear the same moment; the guest adds a song and it appears in both queues; the host pauses/skips and the guest follows within about a second; ending the room ends it for both; with **Let people I follow join my room** on and two accounts that follow each other, the host's row on the friend's Home and the host's `/u/<handle>` show **Join**, which opens the room, and it goes within a few minutes of the room ending; **360** both cards fit and Start is not clipped. Then the jam, with two real phones side by side: (a) the guest taps **Pause for everyone** - both phones stop and the host sees "<guest> paused"; **Play for everyone** starts both again; (b) the guest adds a song - it lands in both queues with the guest's name ("You" on the guest); the guest takes it out and it leaves the host's queue; a song the guest did not add has no cross; (c) the guest taps **Next song** - both move on; (d) the guest turns off **Play on this phone** - the guest's phone goes quiet and the Sync check reads "Not playing here (remote only)", but the guest stays in the room and in the host's People list; the guest's Pause/Play for everyone still works the host's speaker while the guest's phone stays quiet; turned back on, the guest plays again in step; (e) the host turns off **Guests can control playback** - the guest's for-everyone buttons are disabled and Vote to skip appears; (f) the host removes the guest (Remove <name> from the session, then Remove) - the guest sees "<host> removed you from this session" and drops out of People; (g) with someone on an app older than 1.14 in the room, "<name>'s VinaX is out of date" shows.
11. *Search and TV (B, C, D):* a misspelt artist still returns results; with the field empty trending searches appear and tapping one runs it; D: the remote's direction keys reach every control on Home, Search and the player.
12. *Casting (A, B, a TV on the same Wi-Fi):* B: Connect to a device > **Cast to a TV** opens the browser's picker and finds the TV; A: the same row opens the phone's list of TVs; pick it - the song carries on there from the same spot and the phone goes quiet; the TV button in Now Playing reads "Casting to <name>"; pause, play and seek from the TV remote - the phone's player follows within a few seconds and does not jump to the next song; skip on the phone - the TV plays the next song; Stop casting from the TV's card - the phone's sound comes back at its own volume; a phone without Play services shows no casting and nothing breaks.
13. *Karaoke vocals (A, C):* open Karaoke on a song with synced lyrics; Vocals **Low** - the voice drops, the music stays balanced left and right; **Off** - the voice is almost gone, bass and drums stay; **Full** - the song is back as it was; leave karaoke - normal playback sounds unchanged; while casting the pills are off and say why.
14. *Links and Share to VinaX (A):* tap a shared song, playlist and invite link (www) in a chat app - on Android 12+ it opens straight in VinaX with no chooser and lands on that page; a Home or sign-in link stays in the browser; from another app share a song link, a single song name and a list of several lines to VinaX - with the app closed and again with it open - the link opens its page, the name opens Search with it, the list opens Import a playlist filled in; playback does not stop.
15. *Maintenance switch (owner, last):* switch the site to maintenance with a message - within about a minute site and app show the maintenance screen and the console still works; switch back - everything returns within about a minute without a manual refresh.

## Listener guide

A condensed guide to the product as a listener sees it (the same material is in the app: **Help** has seven live tutorials on the real screens - Play your first song, Find any song, Listen Together, Inside a room, Change the app's look, Settings worth knowing, Save and organise - searchable how-tos (among them Look around, then sign in; Theme, glass and size; Flow: music videos, like reels; A new phone or computer), FAQs (including I forgot my password) and the shortcut list; Help > **Replay the welcome tour** replays the welcome). VinaX is free: no subscription, no premium tier, no ads; sign in with Google or with email and a password.

### Getting started

The welcome shows "Step n of N"; the first two steps are required, then Skip / Skip the rest. Steps: **Your name** (>= 2 letters) and **Username** (3-20 letters, numbers, underscores; availability checked while typing, suggestions offered; other listeners find you by it); **Choose your languages** (More languages, All languages; a pre-ticked box offers anonymous usage sharing); **Tap songs you like** (saved to Favorites on this device); four slides (Four places to go, Tap one song, Change it any time, Yours to keep; last offers Live walkthrough). Step 1 also offers **Have a VinaX backup file? Import a file**. Everything can be changed in Settings. **Browse before you sign in** (web): **Explore VinaX first** on the sign-in page (and **Explore** in the header of published pages) -> Discover, Charts, Search and entity pages; play/like/save ask for sign-in right there. **Getting around:** on phones the tab bar (Home, Search, Flow, Library, VinaX AI); from 768px the window bar (Home, the search pill with Browse, Radio, Flow, VinaX AI) and Your Library on the left; `Ctrl/Cmd + K` opens the command palette. **First song:** before anything has played Home shows **All the music you love. Free.** with **Start listening** and **Pick your languages**; afterwards press play on the mix card (the VinaX Mix), start Radio, or tap any song (five more are lined up, familiar first; songs you queue play before those). Heart saves to favourites. **iPhone/iPad:** no store app - Safari > Share > Add to Home Screen > Add (the bar says **How**); opens full-screen, keeps playing with the screen off, lock-screen controls; sound effects (equalizer, balance, mono, loudness) are not available in Safari there. **A Home built for you:** your device arranges Home from what you play, the time of day and the shelves you use; Home never rearranges while you look - it rebuilds on pull to refresh, **Refresh Home**, or after half an hour; Settings > Home > **Rebuild Home now**; **Customise Home** and owner-published layouts always win. **Make it comfortable** in Settings > Appearance: Theme (System, Light, Dark), Glass effect, Background blur, Display size, Density, High contrast, Reduce motion; **App language** English/Telugu/Hindi/Tamil for the menus, Settings, the player, Now Playing, Search, the welcome, the sign-in pages, messages, Help and the guided tours (song names and catalogue text stay as they are). **Keep your music safe:** your library follows your account; Settings > Your data > Back up and restore > export a backup before clearing browser data if you want a file too.

### Player and queue

Tap a song: it starts and VinaX adds the next five (more as the queue runs low). *Why this song?* in the song menu. To follow the list you tapped: turn off Settings > Recommendations > Smart Queue > Build every queue (or Smart Queue on the Queue page). Queue languages: Settings > Recommendations > Languages. **Queue a song yourself:** ⋯ > **Play next** / **Add to queue**; right-click gives a short menu; swipe a row right = add to queue, left = Listen Later; your songs stay when VinaX rebuilds its picks. **Radio** (Home > Radio, or **Start radio** on an empty queue): pick a mood (Melody, Romantic, Dance, Chill, Sad, Devotional, DJ remix, Folk, Beats, Classics, New), a station, or a song/artist; every song menu and artist page has **Start radio**. **Queue page** (Queue button in mini player/Now Playing): **Played** at the top (the songs already played, oldest first: tap one to play it again and the songs after it are Up next again, ⋯ for its song menu, **Clear** with Undo; folded when it holds more than 5 songs and remembers open or folded for the session; a Listen Together guest does not see it), then Now playing, then Up next in three parts like Spotify: **Next in queue** (songs you added: they play right after the current song, in the order you added them; **Clear queue** removes only these), **Next from: <album, playlist or page>** (the rest of what you pressed play on) and **Autoplay** (what VinaX picks once that ends). **Shuffle** reorders the list on screen, so the queue always shows what plays next and Back goes to the song before; turning it off puts the list back in order. Rows are marked **VinaX pick** (replaced by Refresh up next), **Added by you**, none (came from the list you tapped); drag handle (or focus + up/down arrows) reorders; ⋯ per row: Keep this song, Move up/down, Clear from here down, Remove from queue; sort chips Energy, Calm first, Newest, Classics, Mood arc; **Save as playlist**; **Build a queue**; Undo after an accidental remove; **Add to queue** only counts the playing song and Up next as already queued, so a song already played can be queued again. **Smart Queue** switch: on = VinaX builds what plays next around each song you start and songs you add play first; off = your order. **Tune this queue** (Queue page, Now Playing > Up next, or Now Playing > Player tools): More energetic, More chill, More romantic, More melody, More beats, Devotional, DJ remix, Folk, Heartbreak, More classics, More new, Same language, Switch language, Surprise me; added songs stay; the tune lasts until you start a fresh song. **Never repeat** (Settings > Recommendations or the Queue chip): every song VinaX adds is one you have never heard in any version; your own songs and **Play again** always play; VinaX remembers every song played on this device even with the mode off; when new songs run out it looks further (related artists, deeper into the sound, popular in your languages), then says "You've heard everything we can find here. Try Discover or another language" and stops; the heard list is in your backup, **Reset app state** erases it. **Now Playing:** swipe artwork = next/previous; double-tap edges = +/-10 s; double-tap centre = like; tabs Up next / Lyrics / Credits; **Lyrics** full screen; **Player tools** (volume, Speed, Loop a passage, Marks, Tune this queue, More, Share this moment, Ambient mode after 45 s idle); ⋯ menu (Drive mode, Disco lights, Karaoke when synced lyrics exist, Listen together, Share now-playing card); **Karaoke:** the synced lyrics big on screen, with **Vocals** Full, Low or Off above the seek bar (Low turns the voice down, Off takes out most of it and keeps bass and drums; a song recorded in mono goes almost silent; not on iPhone or iPad Safari, without Web Audio, or while casting, and the line under the pills says why; leaving karaoke puts the voice back); **Disco lights:** the screen becomes a light show on the song's beat (tap the lights to hide the controls and fill the whole screen; Adjust: Party, Spotlights, Lasers or Wash, colours from the song, RGB or another set, brightness up to 150%, beat sensitivity, motion speed, and Flash on strong beats, off by default); the flashlight button flashes the phone's flashlight on the beat (Android app, Chrome on Android, Safari on recent iPhones); in the Android app the screen goes to full brightness while the lights are open; where the beat can't be heard (iPhone Safari, casting) the lights keep a steady rhythm and **Tap the beat** sets it; **Artwork / Video** chip when the song has a music video; ⤢ full-screen video canvas (tap = controls, double-tap = like with a heart, quality chip e.g. "540p - Auto", ⤢ browser full screen, chevron/Esc/Android back to leave). **Streaming quality** (Settings > Sound > Audio quality): Auto follows the connection (Very high 320 kbps on Wi-Fi, High 160 on 4G, Medium 96 on 3G, Low 48 on 2G or data saver; carries on from the same spot when the connection changes) or pin Low/Medium/High/Very high; the player's ⋯ > Quality...; downloads have their own **Download quality**. **Loudness normalisation** (Settings > Sound, with sound effects on): every song plays at about the same volume without being squashed; a new song settles in its first few seconds and is remembered on this device (last 500 songs), so a replay is level from the start. **Crossfade** (Settings > Playback > Crossfade, Crossfade length): the last seconds of a song play over the start of the next, one falling while the other rises; on iPhone Safari, with sound effects or disco lights on, or with a sleep timer or loop running the song only fades out, and there is none in Listen Together, while casting, in karaoke or before a podcast episode. **Sleep timer:** 15m, 30m, 60m, End of song, or after 3/5/10 songs; last 30 s fade; Cancel stops it. **Resume where you left off** (songs > 60 s, stopped after 20 s and before the last 20 s; last 80 songs on this device; Settings > Playback > Resume playback; a podcast episode keeps its place however much music you play after it); after a phone call in the Android app playback resumes by itself. **History:** Library > History (last 150 plays; search; filter Today/Yesterday/This week/Last 30 days and language; Clear last hour / today / all; cleared or removed plays also leave Your VinaX).

### Discovery modes

Settings > Recommendations opens with **What VinaX thinks you like** (Pick languages & artists; See the full taste profile = Library > Taste profile). **Discovery** (How far to roam): **Familiar** (about 5% of a queue someone new), **Balanced** (about 20%), **Discover** (about 45%, Home adds new languages); shares are approximate. **Trending vs. your taste** slider. **Languages:** Queue languages -> *Your languages* (playing song's language leads, others follow, never two switches in a row) or *One language*; **Pinned languages** (favoured everywhere including search); **Muted languages** (never recommended; muting unpins). **Smart Queue** > Build every queue. **Steer from a song menu:** More like this, Less like this... (7/14/30 days), Snooze this song... (7/14/30 days), Why this song?, Not interested (hides), Never play *artist* (blocks); each shows Undo. **Hearing less** lists Playing less of (end early, Unmute all), Never play, Kid mode. **Fewer repeats** next to Refresh Home rebuilds Home once without what you heard or were shown lately (memory of up to 600 songs, forgets after 45 days). **Start over:** Settings > Recommendations > Start over > Reset taste profile (also on the Taste profile page); everything on the device: Settings > Your data > Clear and reset > Reset app state (back up first).

### Search and Discover

Search suggests as you type (↑/↓ move, Enter picks, Esc closes); tap a song to play, an artist/album to open, Enter or a suggested search for full results. Results: **Top result**, **Songs**, **Artists**, **Albums**, **Playlists** (See all); filters All/Songs/Albums/Artists/Playlists; Songs sort by relevance, popularity, newest, longest, shortest, A to Z; **Play all** / **Queue all**; *Did you mean ...?*; pinned languages sit a little higher. **Search by lyrics:** type a line (five words or more work best), **Search by lyrics** -> **Songs with those words**; **Search titles instead** goes back. **Voice search** button when speech recognition is supported. Before typing: **Recent searches** (pin up to five: pin button, or long-press on touch), **Trending searches**, **Try searching**, **Trending now**, **Browse all**. **Browse all** grid (three large tiles then smaller ones): Charts, Languages, Moods, then New Releases, Top Playlists, Top Artists, Radio, Movies, Videos, Podcasts, Made for you, Your week; **Any mood, your language** below. **Discover** (sidebar, or **See all** on Browse all): the same grid, **Surprise album** at the top, Flow, the language hubs, **Pick a language** (every shelf below follows it: Trending, New this week, **Pick a mood** with its picks and **See all** for the full search, **Decade radio**, **Pick a year**, playlists, movies). **Languages:** opens with **Popular in your region** (the languages played where you are, your own region first; set it in Settings > Region & privacy; hidden while the country is unknown), then every language hub and the languages you want more or less of. Old Explore and Regions links open Discover and Languages, and old Mixes links open Made for you. **Charts** opens with **Most played on VinaX this week** (the top 10 songs signed-in listeners played most in the last 7 days, shown once at least 10 qualify; **Show all** opens Top songs, which lists more of it). **Your week** (`/weekly`): 30 songs new to you, first by artists you have never played, then more from your languages; it refreshes every Monday and is the same list as **New to you this week** in Made for you. **Podcasts:** **Continue your shows** at the top lists the episodes you started (time left on each; tap to carry on); a podcast's page marks each episode **Played** (from 95%) or shows a progress bar and how much is left; episodes never change your taste profile, and after a queue of only episodes VinaX adds no music and says "That was the last episode in your queue".

### The look

VinaX has one look (1.15): black and green, with Your Library on the left, the page in the middle, the Now playing view on the right and a player bar across the bottom; 1.15.1 adds **Theme** (System, Light, Dark; System follows the device) and glass panels over the playing song's colour. Settings > Appearance: **Theme**, **Glass effect** (Solid … Glass), **Background blur**, **Display size**, **Density**, **High contrast**, **Reduce motion**; Help tutorial **Make it easy to read**. There is no accent colour choice. Troubleshooting: the right-hand panel is missing = the window is narrower than 1280px, or it was closed (press the panel switch at the right of the player bar); the queue opened in the right panel instead of a page = that is how wide screens show it (the panel's "Open the full queue" opens the page).

### Library and backup

**Library** shortcuts: Liked songs, Listen later, Downloads, History, Your VinaX (listening in numbers over **4 weeks**, **6 months** or **All time**: the plays this device logged plus your synced history, each once, so plays on your other devices count too; Most played, Top artists, Languages; a weekly report, a 12-week listening calendar, a daily goal ring; a note says how far back your plays on record go; **What shapes your mixes** opens the Taste profile), Taste profile (**What shapes your mixes**: Languages in your mixes, Artists in your mixes and When you listen, the weights behind Made for you, Home and Radio; **Your plays and stats** goes back to Your VinaX); then playlists, saved artists/albums, Smart collections (saved rules over your liked songs, playlists, Listen later, history), Listen Together, Recently deleted (deleted playlists stay 7 days), and a **Keep it with you** block (the library syncs with your account) with the Backup Center. Search the library; chips All/Playlists/Artists/Albums (+ tag filter); sort Recently added / A to Z; list/grid; **Create playlist**, **Import from text** (also Library > Import > Open a file for CSV/M3U/plain text, or share a list of songs to VinaX from another app); **Listen later** from the song menu (swipe left on touch); playlist menu **Remove duplicates**, **Shuffle play**; every playlist ends with **Recommended songs** (about 10 catalogue songs like the ones in it, never ones already in it, hidden songs, blocked artists or explicit songs in Kid mode; tap a row to play from it, **+** adds it with Undo, **Refresh** brings a new set; it loads when you scroll to it); the **Downloaded** chip (Android). **Backup:** Settings > Your data > Back up and restore > **Export a backup** (or Backup Center > Export a backup now); the file is not encrypted. **Restore:** Backup Center > Open > Choose a backup file... (nothing changes yet) > review each category vs the device > **How to apply** Merge or Replace > untick categories > optionally **Download a safety copy first** > press Merge/Replace; files over 8 MB or from a newer VinaX are refused; **Undo that restore** in the same tab; **Restore a backup (quick)** replaces without preview. A backup never changes your username; it comes with your account when you sign in. **A new phone or computer:** sign in with the same account (Google, or email and password) and your library, playlists, history, taste profile and settings come back; or restore a backup file (Backup Center, or **Import a file** on the welcome). Downloaded songs stay on the device that saved them. **Erase:** Clear history / Clear favorites (Undo), Clear queue, Reset app state; Clear cached metadata under Storage; Reset taste profile; downloads are removed from Library > Downloads.

### Flow

Music videos, one per screen, like reels: **Flow** on the phone tab bar and in the sidebar, or its card on Home or Discover. The feed is only in your languages, each video starts from the beginning, and when one ends the next starts; each visit opens on different videos, and ones you have watched come back only when nothing new is left. Move: swipe up (scroll or ↓ on a computer); tap pauses; double-tap likes the song; the sound button (or `M`) turns sound on or off. Actions: **Play the song** (sends it to the music player), **Queue**, **Share** (sends a `/flow?v=<id>` link, or copies it, that opens Flow on that video). A card on the Videos page, a shared link and an old `/video/<id>` link (now a redirect) open Flow on that video, even outside your languages, with the usual feed after it; if it cannot be loaded Flow says "That video couldn't be loaded. Here are others." and carries on. A shared video link opens signed out; **Play the song**, a like and **Queue** ask the visitor to sign in first and then go ahead. Your music pauses while a video plays and stays where it was. During Listen Together Flow leaves the music alone and videos start muted.

### Listen Together

Open from Library > Listen Together, the player menu > Listen together, the command palette, or Home's first-visit tile. **Tours:** a first visit shows a "Take the one-minute tour" card (**Show me how**, **Not now**) for the "Listen Together" tour (what it is, hosting, joining by code, QR or invite link); once the tour is done or **Not now** is tapped the hero has **How it works** (**Replay the tour** once done) instead. In a live room **Show me around** starts "Inside a room", which points at the room's real controls and skips the ones your role does not have; from Help it asks you to start or join a room first. The player's Together tab links to the first tour. **Host:** Start a session -> 6-character code, QR code, invite link (**Share invite**, **Copy code**); play as usual from any page; only the host sees names, guests see a count; the room page shows the song, **Up next**, listener count. **Join:** open the invite link, or type the code under **Join a session** and press **Join**; your player follows the host (same song and position: it starts where the host is and then stays within a couple of hundredths of a second by playing a touch faster or slower; VinaX adds no songs of its own while you follow). If your phone still sounds late or early next to the host's (every speaker, and above all a Bluetooth one, takes its own time to sound), use **Fine-tune this phone** in the room: **Earlier** or **Later** in 10 ms steps, remembered on that phone; status **Live - in sync / syncing... / catching up...**. **Tap to start listening** appears when the browser wants a tap before sound (e.g. an invite link). The session survives navigation and a tab reload; a pill on every other page shows it - "Hosting - 3 listening", "Listening with Asha", "Asha went quiet" (nothing for 90 s; music keeps playing), "Reconnecting...", "Tap to start listening"; tap it to return, or drag it anywhere on the screen (it stays where you leave it). **Instant:** while the status shows **Instant**, what the host does (play, pause, seek, next song) is heard on every phone at once, and chat, typing and reactions show as they happen; without it the room still works, a couple of seconds behind. **Add songs and react:** guests **Add a song for everyone** (appears in both queues with "Added by <name>"; the host is told if unavailable); the host **Add to the queue**; emoji reactions under **React to the music** rise over the whole screen on every phone, on any page, with the sender's name. **Room energy:** every reaction fills the bar a little and it fades when the room goes quiet; fill it together and the whole room goes off, everyone's emoji across every screen at the same moment. **Leave or end:** guest **Leave session**; host **End for everyone** (guests told); a guest closing the tab leaves; a host closing the tab leaves the room open (guests see the host went quiet; within a day the host can type the code under Join a session to carry on). Rooms also show everyone's name, a **Chat** (last 40 lines; your lines on the right, "Asha is typing" while someone types, a line that could not be sent says so and can be tapped to try again) and **⏭** (host skips; guests vote - the song moves on once more than half the room has voted) and **Save this session as a playlist**.

### Friends and sharing
