**Public profile** (Settings > Profile > Public profile): handle (3-20 letters/digits/underscores) and an optional line, **Create profile**; page at `/u/<handle>` shows your Google name and picture, line, top artists and languages, recent likes (worked out on your device and sent every half hour while the app is open); **Visible to everyone** on at creation (off = only mutual followers); **Share what I'm playing** shows friends the song for a few minutes after you play it (off by default); **Let people I follow join my room** (off by default): while you host Listen Together, people you follow who follow you back see **Join** on Home's Friends are listening and **Join their room** on your profile (only while the room is live); **Remove my profile** deletes the page and every follow both ways. **Following and Blend:** open a profile and **Follow**; followed people who share what they play, or host a room they let you join, appear in **Friends are listening** on Home; **Blend** saves a playlist "Blend with <name>" in your Library, built on your device from up to 10 of their recent likes and 10 of yours: songs you both like first, then their side and yours in turn (up to 50). Their profile then shows **Your blend** with whose likes each song came from (From <name>, From you, Both of you, Added by you), when it was updated, **Refresh** and **Show all**; the button becomes **Play blend**. It rebuilds by itself when you open their profile after either of you liked something new, and songs you add to it by hand stay. **Shared playlists** (playlist ⋯ > Share...): *public page* (anyone with the link can play and save), *invite friends to add songs* (invite link, no account needed, their name shows beside what they added; only you remove songs), the shared copy updates itself a few seconds after you change the playlist (**Update the shared copy** does it now; their additions come into your playlist first), **Stop sharing**. The link follows your account and your backup, so another device or a restore keeps it; **Get the invite link** fetches the invite link on a device that does not have it yet, and on a device without the link **Use the link you made before** re-links a shared copy with the same name. **Discover 5:** five unheard songs a day (Keep -> Listen Later, Skip, Play; swipe right/left on phones; a new five next day). **Songs like this** (⋯ or Player tools) queues ten similar songs; every automatic pick says why in Up next. **Release alerts:** Follow an artist and VinaX checks twice a day; new songs appear in **New from your artists** on Home and as an Android notification (VinaX reads each artist's newest songs, so a release shows on the day it comes out; when the catalogue cannot sort by newest it falls back to the artist's top songs, which may only catch a release once it has some plays; the first check of an artist only learns what is there); **Following** (`/following`) has two tabs: **Artists** gathers followed artists, their new songs newest first (Dismiss each), current top songs, **Check for new songs**, **Play new songs**; **People** (`/following?tab=people`, also Settings > Profile > **People you follow**) lists everyone you follow with their picture, name and handle (and the song while they share it), a link to their profile, **Join** when they host a room they let you into, and **Unfollow** with Undo; **Find by handle** opens a profile from a handle, @handle or a pasted profile link. **More:** Focus sounds (Player tools: rain, wind, deep hum, even hush with a timer), **Match the song** (Settings > Sound: EQ preset follows the song), Drive Mode voice ("play Srivalli", "next", "pause", "play something in Telugu"), playlist import, Smart downloads (Android: this week's Weekly Mix on Wi-Fi only).

### VinaX AI

Needs sign-in (Google, or email and password); open from the side menu (computer), profile picture menu (phone) or `Ctrl/Cmd+K` > "VinaX AI"; music keeps playing (small player at the top; **Back to VinaX**). **Ask:** `Enter` sends, `Shift+Enter` new line; **Stop** ends a reply; six starters (Write, Learn, Code, Visualise, Quiz me, Music) never send. **Look (2026-11-16):** VinaX AI has its own Claude-style look apart from the music app's styles (the last section of `styles/pages/vinaxai.css`, scoped to `.vx-ai`; high contrast keeps the app palette): warm charcoal (dark) or soft cream (light) with a clay accent, serif replies and greeting, solid surfaces instead of glass, a centred message box with the greeting above it, a **Share** button with its label, and on a computer the closed sidebar becomes a slim rail of icons (open sidebar, New chat, Projects, Artifacts, back to music, account). **Thinking... 4s** shows at once with a timer; the model's reasoning streams into a small box under it (tap to fold or open any time), and folds into **Thought for N s** when the answer starts; tap it to read the reasoning again. Under each reply: Copy, Retry (Auto or another model), Read aloud, **Good reply** / **Bad reply** (thumbs; a thumbs down asks what went wrong, with an optional note; press the same thumb again to take it back), Continue (when cut short), the model's name, how hard it thought, the time. Closing the page in the middle of a reply keeps what was written so far (if that save does not get through, it is finished the next time you open VinaX AI). **Ask about this song:** the spark button on the small player at the top puts "Tell me about <song> by <artist>" in the message box for you to send or change. Code blocks have Wrap, Download and Copy. **Edit** a question to send a new version (`< 2 / 3 >` moves between versions). **Choose a model:** the menu beside send - **Auto** (default: quick model for everyday questions, a seeing one for images, long memory for long documents/chats, a stronger one for hard problems; moves on if busy) or any model listed by group with badges (see images, PDF, web, thinking, code), filter chips (Recommended, Fast, Reasoning, Code, Vision, Long context), each model's context size, and a search box (greyed = cannot answer now, with the reason); **More models** (specialists); a model you pick is never swapped. **Thinking** (the bulb beside +): **Auto** (quick for simple messages, longer for hard ones), **Fast**, **Standard**, **Extended** (thinks longer and checks its work), **Maximum** (most thinking room and a final review; only some Gemini models; others use the nearest level and say so under the reply). **+ button:** Web search (Auto/On/Off; numbered source chips and a Sources row), Add photos and files, **Create image** (when the owner has it on: describe the picture, press **Create image**; the reply shows it with **Open full size** and **Download**; a daily number per person), Settings. **Files:** photos (PNG, JPEG, WebP, GIF; 10 per message, shrunk on your device), PDFs (30 MB, 500 pages), text/Markdown/CSV/JSON/code (2 MB); 20 files per message, 50 per chat (owner can change); if the model cannot see images a note offers one that can. **Make it yours:** the gear opens settings - General (theme, artifacts, live previews), Personalisation (what to call you, what to know about you, how to reply, reply style Normal/Concise/Explanatory/Formal/Learning; on your device, sent with each message, not saved with chats), Memory. **Memory:** short notes about lasting things across chats ("Is learning Carnatic vocals"); say "remember that..." / "forget that..."; never keeps passwords, codes, card/account numbers, emails, phone numbers, health details or things about others; Settings > Memory lists every note (**Learned from a chat** / **Added by you**), Delete, **Add a memory**, **Forget everything**; **Use memory** off = neither uses nor saves; **Learn from my chats** off = uses but doesn't save new; **Incognito chat** (hat-and-glasses on a new chat) neither uses nor adds; memory follows your account, shared links never include it, deleting a chat keeps notes learned from it. **Code, pages, charts, diagrams:** substantial output opens as a card and a panel (full screen on a phone): **Preview** (runs in a separate locked-down space), **Code**, Copy, Download, versions (`v2 of 2`, `< >`), drag the panel edge on a computer; pages/charts/diagrams also preview inline, **Fix it** asks for a correction. **Artifacts** (sidebar under New chat, or Settings > Your chats > Your artifacts): every artifact your chats made, newest first, filter chips (Pages, Components, Diagrams, Images, Documents, Code), search, **Open chat**. **Voice** (when enabled): *Dictate* (microphone in the message box, ✓ to finish, × to discard, up to two minutes; the words appear for you to check, never sent by themselves), *Read aloud* (code, links, source numbers skipped; tap again to stop; replies written mostly in an Indian script (Hindi, Bengali, Punjabi, Gujarati, Odia, Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, Malayalam) use Gemini's voices, and when none is free it says "Can’t read this language aloud right now"), *Live voice* (with nothing typed, the sound-wave button where Send is; full-screen conversation with captions; talk over it to stop it; mic button mutes; **End**/`Esc` closes; about ten minutes a session; Model, Voice (30 voices), Language pickers work any time and keep one language unless you choose **Match how I speak**; tap the orb to stop mid-sentence; captions button; knows your typed chat, Personalisation, memory and, in a project chat, the project's instructions (not its files); **web search in Live voice** via the globe button / Settings; what was said is saved into the chat, also when you close the page mid-call; sending the call to the background keeps it going); Settings has the microphone with **Test microphone**, Read aloud voice, Live voice/model/language, Search the web in Live voice, Memory, Export all chats, Your artifacts, Shared links. Music pauses while the mic is open and plays on quietly during Read aloud. **Your chats:** sidebar (Starred first, then by day; ☰ on a phone); search by title and content; ⋯ Star, Rename, Move to project..., Delete (disappears at once, removed within a day); chats follow your account to every device; each has its own address; Export VinaX AI chats (Settings > Your data); deleting the account deletes everything. **Projects:** Projects in the sidebar > New project (name, short description); **Instructions** (<= 8,000 characters; every chat in the project follows them, and Live voice the first 2,000) and **Files** (<= 20 files and 200,000 characters of text) that every chat in it reads; start a chat from the project's page; move chats via ⋯ > Move to project...; Delete project removes instructions and files, chats stay unless **Delete them too**; a shared link to a project chat never shows the project's instructions/files. **Share a chat:** Share (arrow next to the gear) > **Create link** - anyone with it can read the chat as it was when made (files by name only; no reasoning or your name); **Update link** after new messages; **Copy link**, **Share...**; **Stop sharing** (Settings > Your chats > Shared links lists all); a signed-in reader can **Continue this chat** in a new chat of their own. **When something goes wrong:** a model "is busy" -> Try with Auto / another model / Retry; "VinaX AI is taking a break" = switched off for a while; "VinaX AI is unavailable right now" = no provider reachable; "Voice is busy right now" -> try later or type; "Preview needs the sandbox host" -> Code/Copy/Download still work; microphone does not start -> allow it in browser/phone settings (update the Android app if offered); "No sound is reaching VinaX AI" -> a silent/virtual microphone (e.g. BlackHole): Settings > Microphone, pick your real mic, Test microphone (in Live voice tap the mic's name). VinaX AI can make mistakes; what you send is passed to an AI provider (the privacy page names them).

### Keyboard shortcuts

Open the list with `?` (any page with the player) or Settings > Help & about > Keyboard shortcuts. Not available in the Android app. Press plain keys (no Shift).

| Key | Action |
| --- | --- |
| `Space` | Play or pause |
| `→` / `←` | Forward / back 10 seconds |
| `↑` / `↓` | Volume up / down 5% |
| `n` / `p` | Next / previous song |
| `m` | Mute or unmute |
| `s` | Shuffle on or off |
| `r` | Cycle repeat |
| `f` | Like or unlike the current song |
| `?` | Show or hide the shortcut list |
| `Ctrl/Cmd + K` | Open or close the command palette (works while typing; ↑↓ move, Enter runs, Esc closes) |
| Search field: `↑` `↓`, `Enter`, `Esc` | Move through suggestions, pick/search, close |
| Now Playing: `Esc` | Leave (first press brings back hidden controls); `←`/`→`, `Home`/`End` on Up next/Lyrics/Credits tabs switch tabs; `↑`/`↓` on a queue row's handle moves it |
| Flow: `↓`/`Page Down`/`j`, `↑`/`Page Up`/`k`, `Space`, `Esc` | Next, previous, pause/play, close |
| Tours: `→`/`Enter`, `←`, `Esc` | Next step, previous, leave |
| Pop-up message: `Esc` | Close / dismiss newest (unless a sheet or menu is open) |
| Settings > Appearance > App style: arrow keys | Move between styles |

Keys are ignored while typing in a text field/area/drop-down and anywhere inside an open dialog; `Space`, `Enter`, arrows, `Home`, `End`, `Page Up/Down` belong to a focused button, link, slider, tab, switch, checkbox or menu item (Space on a button presses it without pausing); Ctrl/Cmd/Alt combinations are left to the browser except the palette. Mouse: right-click a song = short song menu; right-click elsewhere = app shortcuts; `Shift` + right-click, or a right-click on selected text, = the browser's menu (so Copy works). Text on reading pages (lyrics, Privacy, Terms, Help, About, contact, DMCA, page titles) can be selected and copied; a long-press on song rows, cards, artwork, the player and the nav shows no browser menu or callout. Touch: swipe a song row right = add to queue, left = Listen Later; swipe Now Playing artwork = next/previous; double-tap artwork edges = +/-10 s, centre = like; pull down on Home = refresh; swipe a pop-up down = dismiss; long-press a recent search = pin.

### Android

Get the app: open VinaX in the phone browser > Home > **Get the VinaX app** (or **Take VinaX anywhere**) > **VinaX for Android** > **Download APK** > open the file and allow the install. Sign in with the same account and your library comes across; or export a backup in the browser and restore it in the app. The app adds background playback, media notification and lock-screen controls (play, pause, previous, next, seek), a Now Playing widget and a quick-play widget, offline downloads, lock-screen lyrics (setting), push notifications (new song picks, if turned on in Settings > Notifications > Push notifications), haptics (setting); it asks for notification permission at the end of the welcome; if a call or another app pauses the music it resumes when you return within half an hour. **Download songs:** song ⋯ > **Download** ("Saved for offline"); album/playlist **Download** (counts songs); **Remove download** from the menu or Library > Downloads; find offline music in Library > Downloads (Play all, Shuffle play), the **Downloaded** chip, or **Downloaded only** on a page; highest available quality is used; a stalled download gives up. Failure messages: No internet connection (connect and retry), This song isn't available to download (try another version), Couldn't save to your phone (free space), The download stalled (check connection), Download failed (retry). Downloaded audio stays on that phone and is never in a backup. **In a browser or on iPhone/iPad:** song ⋯ > **Download** saves the song in the browser (Library > Downloads shows each size, the space used and free, and **Remove all**); add VinaX to the Home Screen on an iPhone or iPad, because Safari can clear saved songs if VinaX is not opened for a week; a song whose host does not allow it says **Not available to save**; Download all and Smart downloads are Android-only. **Cast to a TV:** Now Playing > Connect to a device > **Cast to a TV** lists the TVs and speakers on your Wi-Fi; once one is nearby a TV button also appears in Now Playing; pick one and the song carries on there while the phone stays quiet; pause, play and seek from the TV's remote too; tap the TV button (or Cast to a TV) again for **Stop casting**; phones without Play services do not show casting. **Updates:** checked at launch and on return; **Update available** sheet: **Update now** (downloads in-app, opens the installer, Android asks to confirm) or **Update later** (stops reminding about that version); manual: Settings > Help & about > App version > **Check for updates**; an unsupported version asks you to update; "One-time reinstall needed" walks you through saving your data file, uninstalling, installing, then Settings > Your data > Restore a backup (quick).

## Documentation conventions

This README is the only Markdown document in the repository. Write for a reader arriving today and describe what the code does now; a version number appears only where a maintainer needs it. If something cannot be checked, leave it out. Every command must exist in a `package.json`, in `frontend/scripts/`, in `backend/scripts/` or in a workflow. No third-party product, company or music-service names - describe the behaviour instead. Exceptions: host names and secret names a maintainer has to type (deployment, operations, secrets sections), package names where a developer must know them, and VinaX AI's narrow exception (AI model and provider names may appear in its model picker, the model label under each reply, the privacy page, the VinaX AI and deployment sections and nowhere else). Fix this document in the same change as the code it describes; when a section becomes a record of the past, shrink it to a line in [History](#history).

## History

Dated records (release write-ups, audits, QA reports, phase plans) that used to live in `docs/history/`, `docs/`, `frontend/docs/` and the repository root were retired in favour of this README; they described the product at the time and are available in git history. What they covered:

- **Rebuild phases (mid-2026, July-August):** a five-phase plan - Phase 1 foundation and audit (architecture decision record, catalogue/content audit, UX/flow audit phone and desktop, technical audit, privacy baseline, design-token foundation later replaced), Phase 2 core architecture and data, Phase 3 experience surfaces, Phase 4 discovery and personalisation (charts and trending pipeline notes), Phase 5 hardening, polish and launch (QA matrix, staged rollout and rollback runbook).
- **Audits and QA (2026-07 to 2026-10):** a pre-release audit of the whole repository at 1.2.0 (July 11), the August 2026 audit and UX programme with its delta audit, final report and production QA report for 4.3.0 (4.0.0-4.3.0), the 7.2 review (every finding with severity, evidence, fix, validation), and the 9.1 review (confirmed causes of repeated recommendations, live-discovery and location gaps, before/after measurements - it led to the exposure ledger).
- **Release write-ups (2026-09):** 5.31.0 verification; 6.0 "Astra" visual system; the search redesign and Operations Workspace (Discovery upgrade); 6.1 backup format, import review, lighter Home and Library tools; 6.2 AI DJ, DJ voice and AI-designed Home shelves; 6.3 arc sequencing, adaptive re-planning and Queue Builder; 6.4 personalisation pipeline audit, event weights and session state; 6.5 the DJ builds every queue, Tune this queue and Home rebuilt per open; 7.0 audit-first release (next-song pipeline, session intent, data-safe restore, a Worker security fix); the "Flow" visual redesign plan.
- **After that (summarised in the sections above):** 9.x/10.x Listen Together moved to an app-level session; 11.0 Worker pages moved to static files to protect the request quota; 13.x/14.0 the clean-sheet database, Google sign-in required, console rebuild, usage sharing on by default and advertising removed; 15.0 the six new app styles, friends/sharing/daily discovery and the 43 festival skins (since removed, with the Festivals page and every festival-timed pick); the V2 line (1.x): VinaX AI, the personal engine, music AI, song reactions, app language, Following; 1.14 removed every AI feature from the music side (AI DJ, AI Up Next order, AI Home Builder, AI Playlist, AI lyrics tools, song generation) and the personal engine, leaving VinaX AI as the only AI.
- Anything needed to operate the product today is kept in the sections above; the old files were not kept up to date and may name features, paths and versions that no longer exist.
